import { router } from "expo-router";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import {
  Alert,
  Pressable,
  SafeAreaView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  View,
} from "react-native";

import { useSupabaseSession } from "../../lib/auth";
import { apiFetch } from "../../lib/backend";
import { supabase } from "../../lib/supabase";

type ShopContext = {
  auth_user_id: string;
  email: string | null;
  shop_id: string;
  role: string;
};

export default function HomeScreen() {
  const { accessToken } = useSupabaseSession();
  const [shopContext, setShopContext] = useState<ShopContext | null>(null);
  const [loadingContext, setLoadingContext] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    async function load() {
      if (!accessToken) {
        setShopContext(null);
        return;
      }

      setLoadingContext(true);
      try {
        const me = await apiFetch<ShopContext>("/me", accessToken, { method: "GET" });
        setShopContext(me);
      } catch (err) {
        const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";
        Alert.alert("Failed to load shop context", message);
      } finally {
        setLoadingContext(false);
      }
    }

    load();
  }, [accessToken]);

  async function handleLogout() {
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.signOut();
    if (error) {
      Alert.alert("Logout failed", error.message);
      return;
    }
    router.replace("/login");
  }

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.safe}>
      <View style={styles.container}>
        <View style={styles.headerCard}>
          <Text style={styles.title}>Ai Vastra</Text>
          <Text style={styles.subtitle}>Shop account</Text>
          <Text style={styles.metaLine}>
            {loadingContext
              ? "Loading shop..."
              : shopContext
                ? `Shop ready - ${shopContext.role}`
                : "Shop context unavailable"}
          </Text>
        </View>

        <Pressable
          style={[styles.navCard, styles.visualizeCard]}
          onPress={() => router.push("/visualize" as any)}
        >
          <View style={styles.navCardBadge}>
            <Text style={styles.navCardBadgeText}>1</Text>
          </View>
          <Text style={styles.navCardTitle}>Visualize</Text>
          <Text style={styles.navCardSubtitle}>
            Select hero image and fabric, then generate the preview
          </Text>
        </Pressable>

        <Pressable
          style={[styles.navCard, styles.heroCard]}
          onPress={() => router.push("/hero-images" as any)}
        >
          <View style={styles.navCardBadge}>
            <Text style={styles.navCardBadgeText}>2</Text>
          </View>
          <Text style={styles.navCardTitle}>Hero Image</Text>
          <Text style={styles.navCardSubtitle}>
            Create folders and manage the hero image library
          </Text>
        </Pressable>

        <Pressable
          style={[styles.navCard, styles.historyCard]}
          onPress={() => router.push("/output-history" as any)}
        >
          <View style={styles.navCardBadge}>
            <Text style={styles.navCardBadgeText}>3</Text>
          </View>
          <Text style={styles.navCardTitle}>Output History</Text>
          <Text style={styles.navCardSubtitle}>
            View outputs, download results, and open carousel mode
          </Text>
        </Pressable>

        <View style={styles.footerRow}>
          <Pressable style={styles.secondaryBtn} onPress={handleLogout}>
            <Text style={styles.secondaryBtnText}>Logout</Text>
          </Pressable>

          <Pressable
            style={styles.ghostBtn}
            onPress={() => router.push("/dev-upload")}
          >
            <Text style={styles.ghostBtnText}>Dev Tools</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>
      </View>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

import { ScrollViewStyleReset } from 'expo-router/html';

// This file is web-only and used to configure the root HTML for every
// web page during static rendering.
// The contents of this function only run in Node.js environments and
// do not have access to the DOM or browser APIs.
export default function Root({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <html lang="en">
      <head>
        <meta charSet="utf-8" />
        <meta httpEquiv="X-UA-Compatible" content="IE=edge" />
        <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1, shrink-to-fit=no" />

        {/* 
          Disable body scrolling on web. This makes ScrollView components work closer to how they do on native. 
          However, body scrolling is often nice to have for mobile web. If you want to enable it, remove this line.
        */}
        <ScrollViewStyleReset />

        {/* Using raw CSS styles as an escape-hatch to ensure the background color never flickers in dark-mode. */}
        <style dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: responsiveBackground }} />
        {/* Add any additional <head> elements that you want globally available on web... */}
      </head>
      <body>{children}</body>
    </html>
  );
}

const responsiveBackground = `
body {
  background-color: #fff;
}
@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark) {
  body {
    background-color: #000;
  }
}`;

=== D:\AiVastraReactJSDemo 2.2 Codex\references\mobile-backend\mobile\app\carousel.tsx ===
import { router } from "expo-router";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useFocusEffect } from "@react-navigation/native";
import {
  ActivityIndicator,
  Image,
  Pressable,
  SafeAreaView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  View,
} from "react-native";

import { useSupabaseSession } from "../lib/auth";
import { apiFetch } from "../lib/backend";

type GenerationRow = {
  id: string;
  status: string;
  output_path: string | null;
  created_at: string;
};

type DownloadUrlResponse = {
  generation_id: string;
  download_url: string;
  expires_in_seconds: number;
};

const AUTO_ADVANCE_MS = 3500;

export default function CarouselScreen() {
  const { accessToken } = useSupabaseSession();

  const [rows, setRows] = useState<GenerationRow[]>([]);
  const [previewUrls, setPreviewUrls] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [statusText, setStatusText] = useState("Loading outputs...");

  const visibleRows = useMemo(
    () => rows.filter((row) => row.status === "done" && !!row.output_path),
    [rows]
  );

  const currentRow = visibleRows[index] ?? null;
  const currentUrl = currentRow ? previewUrls[currentRow.id] ?? null : null;

  async function getDownloadUrl(token: string, generationId: string) {
    const response = await apiFetch<DownloadUrlResponse>(
      `/generations/${generationId}/download-url`,
      token,
      { method: "GET" }
    );
    const join = response.download_url.includes("?") ? "&" : "?";
    return `${response.download_url}${join}cb=${Date.now()}`;
  }

  async function loadOutputs() {
    if (!accessToken) return;
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      setPreviewUrls({});
      const generations = await apiFetch<GenerationRow[]>(
        "/generations?status=done&limit=100",
        accessToken,
        { method: "GET" }
      );
      setRows(generations);
      setIndex(0);
      setStatusText(
        generations.length
          ? "Carousel ready"
          : "No outputs yet. Generate images first."
      );
    } catch (err) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";
      setStatusText(`Failed to load outputs: ${message}`);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!accessToken) {
      setRows([]);
      setPreviewUrls({});
      return;
    }
    loadOutputs();
  }, [accessToken]);

  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      if (!accessToken) return;
      loadOutputs().catch(() => {
        // state handled in loader
      });
    }, [accessToken])
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!accessToken || !visibleRows.length) return;

    const missing = visibleRows.filter((row) => !previewUrls[row.id]);
    if (!missing.length) return;

    Promise.all(
      missing.map(async (row) => {
        try {
          const url = await getDownloadUrl(accessToken, row.id);
          return { id: row.id, url };
        } catch {
          return null;
        }
      })
    )
      .then((results) => {
        const valid = results.filter(
          (item): item is { id: string; url: string } => !!item
        );
        if (!valid.length) return;
        setPreviewUrls((prev) => {
          const next = { ...prev };
          for (const item of valid) next[item.id] = item.url;
          return next;
        });
      })
      .catch(() => {
        // best effort
      });
  }, [accessToken, visibleRows, previewUrls]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (visibleRows.length <= 1) return;

    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      setIndex((prev) => ((prev + 1) % visibleRows.length));
    }, AUTO_ADVANCE_MS);

    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [visibleRows.length]);

  function goPrevious() {
    if (!visibleRows.length) return;
    setIndex((prev) => (prev - 1 + visibleRows.length) % visibleRows.length);
  }

  function goNext() {
    if (!visibleRows.length) return;
    setIndex((prev) => (prev + 1) % visibleRows.length);
  }

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.safe}>
      <View style={styles.container}>
        <View style={styles.headerRow}>
          <Text style={styles.title}>Carousel Mode</Text>
          <Pressable style={styles.closeBtn} onPress={() => router.back()}>
            <Text style={styles.closeBtnText}>Close</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>

        <Text style={styles.subtitle}>
          {statusText}
          {visibleRows.length > 1 ? ` - Auto-advance ${AUTO_ADVANCE_MS / 1000}s` : ""}
        </Text>

        <View style={styles.viewer}>
          {loading ? (
            <View style={styles.center}>
              <ActivityIndicator color="#fff" />
              <Text style={styles.helperText}>Loading outputs...</Text>
            </View>
          ) : !currentRow ? (
            <View style={styles.center}>
              <Text style={styles.helperText}>No output images available.</Text>
            </View>
          ) : currentUrl ? (
            <Pressable style={styles.viewerTouchable} onPress={goNext}>
              <Image source={{ uri: currentUrl }} style={styles.image} resizeMode="contain" />
            </Pressable>
          ) : (
            <View style={styles.center}>
              <ActivityIndicator color="#fff" />
              <Text style={styles.helperText}>Loading image...</Text>
            </View>
          )}
        </View>

        <View style={styles.footerRow}>
          <Pressable style={styles.navBtn} onPress={goPrevious} disabled={!visibleRows.length}>
            <Text style={styles.navBtnText}>Previous</Text>
          </Pressable>
          <View style={styles.counterPill}>
            <Text style={styles.counterText}>
              {visibleRows.length ? `${index + 1} / ${visibleRows.length}` : "0 / 0"}
            </Text>
          </View>
          <Pressable style={styles.navBtn} onPress={goNext} disabled={!visibleRows.length}>
            <Text style={styles.navBtnText}>Next</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>
      </View>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  safe: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: "#020617" },
  container: { flex: 1, padding: 14, gap: 10 },
  headerRow: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    justifyContent: "space-between",
    alignItems: "center",
    gap: 10,
  },
  title: { color: "#f8fafc", fontSize: 22, fontWeight: "700" },
  closeBtn: {
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: "#475569",
    backgroundColor: "#111827",
    borderRadius: 10,
    paddingHorizontal: 12,
    paddingVertical: 8,
  },
  closeBtnText: { color: "#f8fafc", fontWeight: "600", fontSize: 12 },
  subtitle: { color: "#cbd5e1", fontSize: 12 },
  viewer: {
    flex: 1,
    backgroundColor: "#111827",
    borderRadius: 18,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: "#334155",
    overflow: "hidden",
  },
  viewerTouchable: {
    flex: 1,
  },
  image: {
    width: "100%",
    height: "100%",
    backgroundColor: "#111827",
  },
  center: {
    flex: 1,
    alignItems: "center",
    justifyContent: "center",
    gap: 8,
    padding: 20,
  },
  helperText: {
    color: "#cbd5e1",
    textAlign: "center",
  },
  footerRow: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    gap: 8,
  },
  navBtn: {
    flex: 1,
    backgroundColor: "#f8fafc",
    borderRadius: 12,
    paddingVertical: 14,
    alignItems: "center",
  },
  navBtnText: {
    color: "#111827",
    fontWeight: "700",
  },
  counterPill: {
    borderRadius: 999,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: "#334155",
    backgroundColor: "#111827",
    paddingHorizontal: 14,
    paddingVertical: 10,
  },
  counterText: {
    color: "#e2e8f0",
    fontWeight: "600",
    fontSize: 12,
  },
});

=== D:\AiVastraReactJSDemo 2.2 Codex\references\mobile-backend\mobile\app\dev-upload.tsx ===
import { decode } from "base64-arraybuffer";
// import * as FileSystem from "expo-file-system";
import * as FileSystem from "expo-file-system/legacy";
import * as ImagePicker from "expo-image-picker";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  Alert,
  Button,
  Image,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  TextInput,
  View,
} from "react-native";

import { apiFetch } from "../lib/backend";
import { useSupabaseSession } from "../lib/auth";
import { supabase } from "../lib/supabase";

type ShopContext = {
  auth_user_id: string;
  email: string | null;
  shop_id: string;
  role: string;
};

type HeroImageRow = {
  id: string;
  shop_id: string;
  folder_id: string;
  storage_path: string;
  original_filename: string | null;
  mime_type: string | null;
  file_size_bytes: number | null;
  width: number | null;
  height: number | null;
  created_at: string;
};

type FabricImageRow = {
  id: string;
  shop_id: string;
  storage_path: string;
  original_filename: string | null;
  mime_type: string | null;
  file_size_bytes: number | null;
  width: number | null;
  height: number | null;
  created_at: string;
};

type UploadMode = "hero" | "fabric";

function makeRandomSuffix() {
  return Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8);
}

function guessFileExtension(asset: ImagePicker.ImagePickerAsset): string {
  const fromName = asset.fileName?.split(".").pop()?.toLowerCase();
  const fromMime = asset.mimeType?.split("/").pop()?.toLowerCase();

  const raw = fromName || fromMime || "jpg";
  const cleaned = raw.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, "");

  return cleaned || "jpg";
}

export default function IndexScreen() {
  const { accessToken, user } = useSupabaseSession();
  const [folderId, setFolderId] = useState("");

  const [mode, setMode] = useState<UploadMode>("hero");

  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [shopContext, setShopContext] = useState<ShopContext | null>(null);

  const [lastPreviewUri, setLastPreviewUri] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [lastStoragePath, setLastStoragePath] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [lastMetadataResponse, setLastMetadataResponse] = useState<
    HeroImageRow | FabricImageRow | null
  >(null);

  const [logLines, setLogLines] = useState<string[]>([]);

  const canUploadHero = useMemo(
    () => mode === "hero" && folderId.trim().length > 0,
    [mode, folderId]
  );

  function pushLog(message: string) {
    setLogLines((prev) => [
      `${new Date().toLocaleTimeString()} - ${message}`,
      ...prev,
    ]);
  }

  async function refreshContextFromSession(showAlertOnError = true) {
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const activeToken =
        accessToken || (await supabase.auth.getSession()).data.session?.access_token;

      if (!activeToken) {
        throw new Error("No active session. Login from the main app first.");
      }

      const me = await apiFetch<ShopContext>("/me", activeToken, {
        method: "GET",
      });

      setShopContext(me);
      pushLog(`Refreshed /me. shop_id=${me.shop_id}`);
    } catch (err) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";
      pushLog(`Refresh context failed: ${message}`);
      if (showAlertOnError) {
        Alert.alert("Error", message);
      }
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!accessToken) {
      setShopContext(null);
      return;
    }

    refreshContextFromSession(false);
  }, [accessToken]);

  async function pickAndUpload() {
    if (!accessToken) {
      Alert.alert("Not authenticated", "Login from the main app first.");
      return;
    }

    if (!shopContext) {
      Alert.alert("Not ready", "Load shop context from current session first.");
      return;
    }

    if (mode === "hero" && !folderId.trim()) {
      Alert.alert("Folder required", "Enter a folder_id for hero image upload.");
      return;
    }

    setBusy(true);

    try {
      pushLog("Opening image library...");

      const pickerResult = await ImagePicker.launchImageLibraryAsync({
        mediaTypes: ImagePicker.MediaTypeOptions.Images,
        quality: 1,
      });

      if (pickerResult.canceled) {
        pushLog("Image selection canceled.");
        return;
      }

      const asset = pickerResult.assets[0];
      if (!asset?.uri) {
        throw new Error("No image selected");
      }

      setLastPreviewUri(asset.uri);

      const ext = guessFileExtension(asset);
      const filename = `${Date.now()}-${makeRandomSuffix()}.${ext}`;

      const bucket = mode === "hero" ? "hero-images" : "fabric-images";
      const storagePath =
        mode === "hero"
          ? `${shopContext.shop_id}/${folderId.trim()}/${filename}`
          : `${shopContext.shop_id}/${filename}`;

      pushLog(`Reading file from device...`);
      const base64 = await FileSystem.readAsStringAsync(asset.uri, {
        encoding: "base64",
      });

      pushLog(`Uploading to Supabase Storage bucket=${bucket}...`);
      const { error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
        .from(bucket)
        .upload(storagePath, decode(base64), {
          contentType: asset.mimeType || "image/jpeg",
          upsert: false,
        });

      if (uploadError) {
        throw new Error(uploadError.message);
      }

      setLastStoragePath(storagePath);
      pushLog(`Storage upload complete: ${storagePath}`);

      const activeToken =
        accessToken ||
        (await supabase.auth.getSession()).data.session?.access_token;

      if (!activeToken) {
        throw new Error("No access token available for backend metadata call");
      }

      if (mode === "hero") {
        const metadataBody = {
          folder_id: folderId.trim(),
          storage_path: storagePath,
          original_filename: asset.fileName ?? filename,
          mime_type: asset.mimeType ?? "image/jpeg",
          file_size_bytes: asset.fileSize ?? null,
          width: asset.width ?? null,
          height: asset.height ?? null,
        };

        const row = await apiFetch<HeroImageRow>("/hero-images", activeToken, {
          method: "POST",
          body: JSON.stringify(metadataBody),
        });

        setLastMetadataResponse(row);
        pushLog(`Hero metadata saved. id=${row.id}`);
      } else {
        const metadataBody = {
          storage_path: storagePath,
          original_filename: asset.fileName ?? filename,
          mime_type: asset.mimeType ?? "image/jpeg",
          file_size_bytes: asset.fileSize ?? null,
          width: asset.width ?? null,
          height: asset.height ?? null,
        };

        const row = await apiFetch<FabricImageRow>("/fabric-images", activeToken, {
          method: "POST",
          body: JSON.stringify(metadataBody),
        });

        setLastMetadataResponse(row);
        pushLog(`Fabric metadata saved. id=${row.id}`);
      }

      Alert.alert("Success", "Upload + metadata registration completed.");
    } catch (err) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";
      pushLog(`Upload flow failed: ${message}`);
      Alert.alert("Upload failed", message);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.container}>
      <Text style={styles.heading}>Dev Upload Test (Step 19)</Text>
      <Text style={styles.subheading}>
        Temporary screen to validate mobile upload pipeline end-to-end.
      </Text>

      <View style={styles.card}>
        <Text style={styles.label}>App Session (from Step 25 login)</Text>
        <Text style={styles.metaText}>User: {user?.email ?? "Not logged in"}</Text>

        <View style={styles.buttonGap}>
          <Button
            title={busy ? "Working..." : "Load / Refresh /me from session"}
            onPress={() => {
              void refreshContextFromSession();
            }}
            disabled={busy}
          />
        </View>

        <Text style={styles.metaText}>
          Session: {accessToken ? "Available" : "Not loaded"}
        </Text>
        <Text style={styles.metaText}>
          Shop: {shopContext ? shopContext.shop_id : "Not loaded"}
        </Text>
        <Text style={styles.metaText}>
          Role: {shopContext ? shopContext.role : "-"}
        </Text>
      </View>

      <View style={styles.card}>
        <Text style={styles.label}>Upload Mode</Text>
        <View style={styles.row}>
          <View style={styles.flexButton}>
            <Button
              title={mode === "hero" ? "Hero (Selected)" : "Hero"}
              onPress={() => setMode("hero")}
              disabled={busy}
            />
          </View>
          <View style={styles.flexButton}>
            <Button
              title={mode === "fabric" ? "Fabric (Selected)" : "Fabric"}
              onPress={() => setMode("fabric")}
              disabled={busy}
            />
          </View>
        </View>

        <Text style={styles.label}>Folder ID (required for hero mode)</Text>
        <TextInput
          style={styles.input}
          value={folderId}
          onChangeText={setFolderId}
          placeholder="Paste folder_id from /folders"
          autoCapitalize="none"
        />

        <Text style={styles.metaText}>
          Hero upload ready: {canUploadHero ? "Yes" : "No (needs folder_id)"}
        </Text>

        <View style={styles.buttonGap}>
          <Button
            title={busy ? "Working..." : "Pick Image + Upload + Save Metadata"}
            onPress={pickAndUpload}
            disabled={busy}
          />
        </View>
      </View>

      {lastPreviewUri ? (
        <View style={styles.card}>
          <Text style={styles.label}>Last Selected Image Preview</Text>
          <Image source={{ uri: lastPreviewUri }} style={styles.preview} />
        </View>
      ) : null}

      <View style={styles.card}>
        <Text style={styles.label}>Last Storage Path</Text>
        <Text style={styles.codeText}>{lastStoragePath ?? "-"}</Text>

        <Text style={styles.label}>Last Metadata Response</Text>
        <Text style={styles.codeText}>
          {lastMetadataResponse
            ? JSON.stringify(lastMetadataResponse, null, 2)
            : "-"}
        </Text>
      </View>

      <View style={styles.card}>
        <Text style={styles.label}>Logs</Text>
        <Text style={styles.codeText}>
          {logLines.length ? logLines.join("\n") : "No logs yet"}
        </Text>
      </View>
    </ScrollView>
  );
}

import { router } from "expo-router";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
  Pressable,
  SafeAreaView,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Switch,
  Text,
  TextInput,
  View,
} from "react-native";

import { useSupabaseSession } from "../lib/auth";
import { apiFetch } from "../lib/backend";

type FolderRow = {
  id: string;
  shop_id: string;
  name: string;
  prompt_template: string;
  is_active: boolean;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
};

type FolderCreateRequest = {
  name: string;
  prompt_template: string;
};

type FolderUpdateRequest = Partial<{
  name: string;
  prompt_template: string;
  is_active: boolean;
}>;

export default function FoldersScreen() {
  const { accessToken } = useSupabaseSession();

  const [folders, setFolders] = useState<FolderRow[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  const [includeInactive, setIncludeInactive] = useState(true);

  const [editingFolderId, setEditingFolderId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [promptTemplate, setPromptTemplate] = useState("");
  const [isActive, setIsActive] = useState(true);

  const isEditMode = useMemo(() => !!editingFolderId, [editingFolderId]);

  async function loadFolders() {
    if (!accessToken) return;

    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const rows = await apiFetch<FolderRow[]>(
        `/folders?include_inactive=${includeInactive ? "true" : "false"}`,
        accessToken,
        { method: "GET" }
      );
      setFolders(rows);
    } catch (err) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";
      Alert.alert("Failed to load folders", message);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    loadFolders();
  }, [accessToken, includeInactive]);

  function resetForm() {
    setEditingFolderId(null);
    setName("");
    setPromptTemplate("");
    setIsActive(true);
  }

  function startEdit(folder: FolderRow) {
    setEditingFolderId(folder.id);
    setName(folder.name);
    setPromptTemplate(folder.prompt_template || "");
    setIsActive(folder.is_active);
  }

  async function handleCreateOrUpdate() {
    if (!accessToken) {
      Alert.alert("Not authenticated", "Please login again.");
      return;
    }

    if (!name.trim()) {
      Alert.alert("Missing folder name", "Folder name is required.");
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);
    try {
      if (!isEditMode) {
        const body: FolderCreateRequest = {
          name: name.trim(),
          prompt_template: promptTemplate.trim(),
        };

        await apiFetch<FolderRow>("/folders", accessToken, {
          method: "POST",
          body: JSON.stringify(body),
        });

        Alert.alert("Success", "Folder created.");
      } else {
        const body: FolderUpdateRequest = {
          name: name.trim(),
          prompt_template: promptTemplate.trim(),
          is_active: isActive,
        };

        await apiFetch<FolderRow>(`/folders/${editingFolderId}`, accessToken, {
          method: "PATCH",
          body: JSON.stringify(body),
        });

        Alert.alert("Success", "Folder updated.");
      }

      resetForm();
      await loadFolders();
    } catch (err) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";
      Alert.alert(isEditMode ? "Update failed" : "Create failed", message);
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  function confirmDelete(folder: FolderRow) {
    if (!accessToken) {
      Alert.alert("Not authenticated", "Please login again.");
      return;
    }

    Alert.alert(
      "Delete folder",
      `Delete "${folder.name}"?`,
      [
        { text: "Cancel", style: "cancel" },
        {
          text: "Delete",
          style: "destructive",
          onPress: async () => {
            try {
              setSaving(true);
              await apiFetch<{ deleted: boolean; id: string; name: string }>(
                `/folders/${folder.id}`,
                accessToken,
                { method: "DELETE" }
              );

              if (editingFolderId === folder.id) {
                resetForm();
              }

              await loadFolders();
              Alert.alert("Deleted", `"${folder.name}" was deleted.`);
            } catch (err) {
              const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";
              Alert.alert("Delete failed", message);
            } finally {
              setSaving(false);
            }
          },
        },
      ],
      { cancelable: true }
    );
  }

  if (!accessToken) {
    return (
      <SafeAreaView style={styles.safe}>
        <View style={styles.center}>
          <Text style={styles.title}>Folders</Text>
          <Text style={styles.muted}>No active session. Go back and login.</Text>
          <Pressable style={styles.secondaryBtn} onPress={() => router.replace("/(tabs)")}>
            <Text style={styles.secondaryBtnText}>Back to Home</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>
      </SafeAreaView>
    );
  }

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.safe}>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.container}>
        <View style={styles.headerRow}>
          <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
            <Text style={styles.title}>Folder Management</Text>
            <Text style={styles.muted}>Create categories and prompt templates</Text>
          </View>
          <Pressable style={styles.secondaryBtnSmall} onPress={() => router.back()}>
            <Text style={styles.secondaryBtnText}>Back</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.card}>
          <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>
            {isEditMode ? "Edit Folder" : "Create Folder"}
          </Text>

          <Text style={styles.label}>Folder Name</Text>
          <TextInput
            style={styles.input}
            value={name}
            onChangeText={setName}
            placeholder="e.g. Sarees, Shirts, Plain Suit"
          />

          <Text style={styles.label}>Prompt Template (category-specific)</Text>
          <TextInput
            style={[styles.input, styles.textArea]}
            value={promptTemplate}
            onChangeText={setPromptTemplate}
            placeholder="Preserve model, pose, scene. Change only saree garment fabric to match provided fabric image..."
            multiline
            textAlignVertical="top"
          />

          {isEditMode ? (
            <View style={styles.switchRow}>
              <Text style={styles.label}>Active</Text>
              <Switch value={isActive} onValueChange={setIsActive} disabled={saving} />
            </View>
          ) : null}

          <View style={styles.buttonRow}>
            <Pressable
              style={[styles.primaryBtn, (saving || loading) && styles.disabledBtn]}
              onPress={handleCreateOrUpdate}
              disabled={saving || loading}
            >
              {saving ? (
                <ActivityIndicator color="#fff" />
              ) : (
                <Text style={styles.primaryBtnText}>
                  {isEditMode ? "Update Folder" : "Create Folder"}
                </Text>
              )}
            </Pressable>

            {isEditMode ? (
              <Pressable
                style={styles.secondaryBtn}
                onPress={resetForm}
                disabled={saving || loading}
              >
                <Text style={styles.secondaryBtnText}>Cancel Edit</Text>
              </Pressable>
            ) : null}
          </View>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.card}>
          <View style={styles.listHeaderRow}>
            <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>Folders</Text>

            <View style={styles.inlineControls}>
              <Text style={styles.smallLabel}>Show inactive</Text>
              <Switch
                value={includeInactive}
                onValueChange={setIncludeInactive}
                disabled={loading || saving}
              />
              <Pressable
                style={styles.secondaryBtnSmall}
                onPress={loadFolders}
                disabled={loading || saving}
              >
                <Text style={styles.secondaryBtnText}>Refresh</Text>
              </Pressable>
            </View>
          </View>

          {loading ? (
            <View style={styles.centerRow}>
              <ActivityIndicator />
              <Text style={styles.muted}>Loading folders...</Text>
            </View>
          ) : folders.length === 0 ? (
            <Text style={styles.muted}>No folders yet.</Text>
          ) : (
            <View style={styles.list}>
              {folders.map((folder) => (
                <View key={folder.id} style={styles.folderCard}>
                  <View style={styles.folderTopRow}>
                    <Text style={styles.folderName}>{folder.name}</Text>
                    <View
                      style={[
                        styles.badge,
                        folder.is_active ? styles.badgeActive : styles.badgeInactive,
                      ]}
                    >
                      <Text style={styles.badgeText}>
                        {folder.is_active ? "Active" : "Inactive"}
                      </Text>
                    </View>
                  </View>

                  <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Folder ID</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.codeText}>{folder.id}</Text>

                  <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Prompt Template</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.promptPreview}>
                    {folder.prompt_template?.trim()
                      ? folder.prompt_template
                      : "(No prompt template set)"}
                  </Text>

                  <View style={styles.folderActionRow}>
                    <Pressable
                      style={styles.secondaryBtnSmall}
                      onPress={() => startEdit(folder)}
                      disabled={saving || loading}
                    >
                      <Text style={styles.secondaryBtnText}>Edit</Text>
                    </Pressable>

                    <Pressable
                      style={styles.dangerBtnSmall}
                      onPress={() => confirmDelete(folder)}
                      disabled={saving || loading}
                    >
                      <Text style={styles.dangerBtnText}>Delete</Text>
                    </Pressable>
                  </View>
                </View>
              ))}
            </View>
          )}
        </View>
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

import { decode } from "base64-arraybuffer";
import * as FileSystem from "expo-file-system/legacy";
import * as ImagePicker from "expo-image-picker";
import { router, useLocalSearchParams } from "expo-router";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
  Image,
  Pressable,
  SafeAreaView,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  View,
} from "react-native";

import { useSupabaseSession } from "../lib/auth";
import { apiFetch } from "../lib/backend";
import { supabase } from "../lib/supabase";

type InputSource = "gallery" | "camera";

type ShopContext = {
  auth_user_id: string;
  email: string | null;
  shop_id: string;
  role: string;
};

type FolderRow = {
  id: string;
  shop_id: string;
  name: string;
  prompt_template: string;
  is_active: boolean;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
};

type HeroImageRow = {
  id: string;
  shop_id: string;
  folder_id: string;
  storage_path: string;
  original_filename: string | null;
  mime_type: string | null;
  file_size_bytes: number | null;
  width: number | null;
  height: number | null;
  created_at: string;
};

type FabricImageRow = {
  id: string;
  shop_id: string;
  storage_path: string;
  original_filename: string | null;
  mime_type: string | null;
  file_size_bytes: number | null;
  width: number | null;
  height: number | null;
  created_at: string;
};

type GenerationCreateResponse = {
  id: string;
  status: string;
  credits_used: number;
  balance_before: number;
  balance_after: number;
};

type GenerationDetailResponse = {
  id: string;
  status: string;
  output_path: string | null;
  error: string | null;
};

function makeRandomSuffix() {
  return Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8);
}

function guessFileExtension(asset: ImagePicker.ImagePickerAsset): string {
  const fromName = asset.fileName?.split(".").pop()?.toLowerCase();
  const fromMime = asset.mimeType?.split("/").pop()?.toLowerCase();
  const raw = fromName || fromMime || "jpg";
  return raw.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, "") || "jpg";
}

function isPendingStatus(status?: string | null) {
  return status === "queued" || status === "processing";
}

export default function GenerateScreen() {
  const { accessToken } = useSupabaseSession();
  const params = useLocalSearchParams<{
    pickedHeroImageId?: string;
    pickedHeroFolderId?: string;
    pickedHeroImageNonce?: string;
  }>();

  const [shopContext, setShopContext] = useState<ShopContext | null>(null);
  const [folders, setFolders] = useState<FolderRow[]>([]);
  const [heroImages, setHeroImages] = useState<HeroImageRow[]>([]);

  const [selectedFolderId, setSelectedFolderId] = useState<string>("");
  const [selectedHeroImageId, setSelectedHeroImageId] = useState<string>("");
  const [selectedFabricImage, setSelectedFabricImage] = useState<FabricImageRow | null>(null);

  const [selectedHeroPreviewUrl, setSelectedHeroPreviewUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [selectedFabricPreviewUrl, setSelectedFabricPreviewUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [uploadingFabric, setUploadingFabric] = useState(false);
  const [creatingGeneration, setCreatingGeneration] = useState(false);
  const [visualizingGenerationId, setVisualizingGenerationId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [statusText, setStatusText] = useState("Preparing Visualize screen...");

  const selectedFolder = useMemo(
    () => folders.find((f) => f.id === selectedFolderId) ?? null,
    [folders, selectedFolderId]
  );

  const selectedHero = useMemo(
    () => heroImages.find((h) => h.id === selectedHeroImageId) ?? null,
    [heroImages, selectedHeroImageId]
  );

  async function loadShopContext(token: string) {
    const me = await apiFetch<ShopContext>("/me", token, { method: "GET" });
    setShopContext(me);
    return me;
  }

  async function loadFolders(token: string) {
    const rows = await apiFetch<FolderRow[]>("/folders?include_inactive=true", token, {
      method: "GET",
    });
    setFolders(rows);
    return rows;
  }

  async function loadHeroImagesForFolder(token: string, folderId?: string) {
    if (!folderId) {
      setHeroImages([]);
      return [];
    }

    const rows = await apiFetch<HeroImageRow[]>(
      `/hero-images?folder_id=${encodeURIComponent(folderId)}`,
      token,
      { method: "GET" }
    );
    setHeroImages(rows);
    return rows;
  }

  async function refreshVisualizeData() {
    if (!accessToken) return;

    setLoading(true);
    try {
      setStatusText("Loading shop and hero folders...");
      await loadShopContext(accessToken);
      await loadFolders(accessToken);

      if (selectedFolderId) {
        await loadHeroImagesForFolder(accessToken, selectedFolderId);
      }

      setStatusText("Ready to visualize.");
    } catch (err) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";
      setStatusText(`Load failed: ${message}`);
      Alert.alert("Visualize", message);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!accessToken) {
      setShopContext(null);
      setFolders([]);
      setHeroImages([]);
      setSelectedFolderId("");
      setSelectedHeroImageId("");
      setSelectedFabricImage(null);
      setSelectedHeroPreviewUrl(null);
      setSelectedFabricPreviewUrl(null);
      setVisualizingGenerationId(null);
      return;
    }

    refreshVisualizeData();
  }, [accessToken]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!accessToken || !selectedFolderId) return;
    loadHeroImagesForFolder(accessToken, selectedFolderId).catch((err) => {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";
      setStatusText(`Failed to load hero images: ${message}`);
    });
  }, [accessToken, selectedFolderId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const pickedHeroFolderId =
      typeof params.pickedHeroFolderId === "string" ? params.pickedHeroFolderId : "";
    const pickedHeroImageId =
      typeof params.pickedHeroImageId === "string" ? params.pickedHeroImageId : "";

    if (!pickedHeroFolderId || !pickedHeroImageId) return;

    setSelectedFolderId(pickedHeroFolderId);
    setSelectedHeroImageId(pickedHeroImageId);
    setStatusText("Hero image selected.");
  }, [params.pickedHeroFolderId, params.pickedHeroImageId, params.pickedHeroImageNonce]);

  useEffect(() => {
    async function loadPreview() {
      if (!selectedHero?.storage_path) {
        setSelectedHeroPreviewUrl(null);
        return;
      }

      try {
        const { data, error } = await supabase.storage
          .from("hero-images")
          .createSignedUrl(selectedHero.storage_path, 3600);
        if (error) throw new Error(error.message);

        const signedUrl =
          (data as { signedUrl?: string; signedURL?: string } | null)?.signedUrl ||
          (data as { signedUrl?: string; signedURL?: string } | null)?.signedURL ||
          null;

        setSelectedHeroPreviewUrl(signedUrl);
      } catch {
        setSelectedHeroPreviewUrl(null);
      }
    }

    loadPreview();
  }, [selectedHero?.id, selectedHero?.storage_path]);

  useEffect(() => {
    async function loadPreview() {
      if (!selectedFabricImage?.storage_path) {
        setSelectedFabricPreviewUrl(null);
        return;
      }

      try {
        const { data, error } = await supabase.storage
          .from("fabric-images")
          .createSignedUrl(selectedFabricImage.storage_path, 3600);
        if (error) throw new Error(error.message);

        const signedUrl =
          (data as { signedUrl?: string; signedURL?: string } | null)?.signedUrl ||
          (data as { signedUrl?: string; signedURL?: string } | null)?.signedURL ||
          null;

        setSelectedFabricPreviewUrl(signedUrl);
      } catch {
        setSelectedFabricPreviewUrl(null);
      }
    }

    loadPreview();
  }, [selectedFabricImage?.id, selectedFabricImage?.storage_path]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!accessToken || !visualizingGenerationId) return;
    const token = accessToken;
    const generationId = visualizingGenerationId;

    let cancelled = false;

    async function pollOnce() {
      try {
        const row = await apiFetch<GenerationDetailResponse>(
          `/generations/${generationId}`,
          token,
          { method: "GET" }
        );

        if (cancelled) return;

        if (row.status === "done") {
          setStatusText("Visualization ready.");
          setVisualizingGenerationId(null);
          router.push({
            pathname: "/output-viewer" as any,
            params: { generationId: row.id },
          } as any);
          return;
        }

        if (row.status === "failed") {
          setStatusText(row.error || "Generation failed.");
          setVisualizingGenerationId(null);
          Alert.alert("Visualization failed", row.error || "Please try again.");
          return;
        }

        setStatusText("Visualizing...");
      } catch (err) {
        if (cancelled) return;
        const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";
        setStatusText(`Generation status check failed: ${message}`);
      }
    }

    pollOnce();
    const timer = setInterval(pollOnce, 3000);

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      clearInterval(timer);
    };
  }, [accessToken, visualizingGenerationId]);

  async function ensurePermission(source: InputSource) {
    if (source === "gallery") {
      const res = await ImagePicker.requestMediaLibraryPermissionsAsync();
      if (!res.granted) throw new Error("Media library permission not granted");
      return;
    }

    const res = await ImagePicker.requestCameraPermissionsAsync();
    if (!res.granted) throw new Error("Camera permission not granted");
  }

  async function pickAsset(source: InputSource) {
    if (source === "camera") {
      return ImagePicker.launchCameraAsync({
        mediaTypes: ImagePicker.MediaTypeOptions.Images,
        quality: 1,
      });
    }

    return ImagePicker.launchImageLibraryAsync({
      mediaTypes: ImagePicker.MediaTypeOptions.Images,
      quality: 1,
    });
  }

  async function handleFabricSelection(source: InputSource) {
    if (!accessToken) {
      Alert.alert("Not authenticated", "Please login again.");
      return;
    }

    if (!shopContext) {
      Alert.alert("Not ready", "Shop context is still loading.");
      return;
    }

    setUploadingFabric(true);
    try {
      setStatusText(source === "camera" ? "Opening camera..." : "Opening gallery...");
      await ensurePermission(source);

      const pickerResult = await pickAsset(source);
      if (pickerResult.canceled) {
        setStatusText("Fabric image selection canceled.");
        return;
      }

      const asset = pickerResult.assets[0];
      if (!asset?.uri) throw new Error("No image selected");

      const ext = guessFileExtension(asset);
      const filename = `${Date.now()}-${makeRandomSuffix()}.${ext}`;
      const storagePath = `${shopContext.shop_id}/${filename}`;

      setStatusText("Uploading fabric image...");
      const base64 = await FileSystem.readAsStringAsync(asset.uri, {
        encoding: "base64",
      });

      const { error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
        .from("fabric-images")
        .upload(storagePath, decode(base64), {
          contentType: asset.mimeType || "image/jpeg",
          upsert: false,
        });

      if (uploadError) throw new Error(uploadError.message);

      const row = await apiFetch<FabricImageRow>("/fabric-images", accessToken, {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({
          storage_path: storagePath,
          original_filename: asset.fileName ?? filename,
          mime_type: asset.mimeType ?? "image/jpeg",
          file_size_bytes: asset.fileSize ?? null,
          width: asset.width ?? null,
          height: asset.height ?? null,
        }),
      });

      setSelectedFabricImage(row);
      setStatusText("Fabric image selected.");
    } catch (err) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";
      setStatusText(`Fabric image failed: ${message}`);
      Alert.alert("Fabric Image", message);
    } finally {
      setUploadingFabric(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleCreateGeneration() {
    if (!accessToken) {
      Alert.alert("Not authenticated", "Please login again.");
      return;
    }

    if (!selectedHeroImageId) {
      Alert.alert("Hero image required", "Please select a hero image first.");
      return;
    }

    if (!selectedFabricImage?.id) {
      Alert.alert("Fabric image required", "Please capture or choose a fabric image first.");
      return;
    }

    setCreatingGeneration(true);
    try {
      setStatusText("Creating generation job...");

      const response = await apiFetch<GenerationCreateResponse>("/generations", accessToken, {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({
          hero_image_id: selectedHeroImageId,
          fabric_image_id: selectedFabricImage.id,
        }),
      });

      setVisualizingGenerationId(response.id);
      setStatusText("Visualizing...");
    } catch (err) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";
      setStatusText(`Generate failed: ${message}`);
      Alert.alert("Generate failed", message);
    } finally {
      setCreatingGeneration(false);
    }
  }

  const isVisualizing = !!visualizingGenerationId;
  const actionBusy = loading || uploadingFabric || creatingGeneration || isVisualizing;

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.safe}>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.container}>
        <View style={styles.headerRow}>
          <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
            <Text style={styles.title}>Visualize</Text>
            <Text style={styles.subtitle}>
              Select hero image and fabric image to generate output
            </Text>
          </View>
          <Pressable style={styles.secondaryBtnSmall} onPress={() => router.back()}>
            <Text style={styles.secondaryBtnSmallText}>Back</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.statusCard}>
          <Text style={styles.statusText}>{statusText}</Text>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.card}>
          <View style={styles.cardHeaderRow}>
            <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>Hero Image Selection</Text>
            <Pressable
              style={styles.primaryBtnSmall}
              onPress={() => router.push("/hero-images?picker=1" as any)}
              disabled={actionBusy}
            >
              <Text style={styles.primaryBtnSmallText}>Select Hero Image</Text>
            </Pressable>
          </View>

          {selectedHeroPreviewUrl ? (
            <Image source={{ uri: selectedHeroPreviewUrl }} style={styles.previewTile} />
          ) : (
            <View style={styles.previewPlaceholder}>
              <Text style={styles.previewPlaceholderText}>
                No hero image selected yet
              </Text>
            </View>
          )}

          {selectedFolder?.name ? (
            <Text style={styles.infoText}>Folder: {selectedFolder.name}</Text>
          ) : null}
        </View>

        <View style={styles.card}>
          <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>Fabric Image Selection</Text>

          {selectedFabricPreviewUrl ? (
            <Image source={{ uri: selectedFabricPreviewUrl }} style={styles.previewTile} />
          ) : (
            <View style={styles.previewPlaceholder}>
              <Text style={styles.previewPlaceholderText}>
                Capture or choose a fabric image
              </Text>
            </View>
          )}

          <View style={styles.buttonRow}>
            <Pressable
              style={[styles.secondaryBtn, actionBusy && styles.disabledBtn]}
              onPress={() => handleFabricSelection("camera")}
              disabled={actionBusy}
            >
              <Text style={styles.secondaryBtnText}>Capture</Text>
            </Pressable>

            <Pressable
              style={[styles.secondaryBtn, actionBusy && styles.disabledBtn]}
              onPress={() => handleFabricSelection("gallery")}
              disabled={actionBusy}
            >
              <Text style={styles.secondaryBtnText}>Gallery</Text>
            </Pressable>
          </View>

        </View>

        <View style={styles.card}>
          <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>Generate</Text>

          <Pressable
            style={[styles.generateBtn, actionBusy && styles.disabledBtn]}
            onPress={handleCreateGeneration}
            disabled={actionBusy || !selectedHeroImageId || !selectedFabricImage?.id}
          >
            <Text style={styles.generateBtnText}>
              {creatingGeneration ? "Starting..." : "Generate"}
            </Text>
          </Pressable>

          {isVisualizing ? (
            <View style={styles.visualizingBox}>
              <ActivityIndicator size="large" color="#111827" />
              <Text style={styles.visualizingText}>Visualizing...</Text>
            </View>
          ) : null}
        </View>
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

import { router, useLocalSearchParams } from "expo-router";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
  Pressable,
  SafeAreaView,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  TextInput,
  View,
} from "react-native";

import { useSupabaseSession } from "../lib/auth";
import { apiFetch } from "../lib/backend";

type FolderRow = {
  id: string;
  shop_id: string;
  name: string;
  prompt_template: string;
  is_active: boolean;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
};

export default function HeroImagesFoldersScreen() {
  const { accessToken } = useSupabaseSession();
  const params = useLocalSearchParams<{ picker?: string }>();

  const pickerMode = useMemo(
    () => params.picker === "1" || params.picker === "true",
    [params.picker]
  );

  const [folders, setFolders] = useState<FolderRow[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false);
  const [folderName, setFolderName] = useState("");
  const [showCreateForm, setShowCreateForm] = useState(false);

  async function loadFolders() {
    if (!accessToken) return;

    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const rows = await apiFetch<FolderRow[]>("/folders?include_inactive=true", accessToken, {
        method: "GET",
      });

      rows.sort((a, b) => {
        if (a.is_active !== b.is_active) return a.is_active ? -1 : 1;
        return a.name.localeCompare(b.name);
      });

      setFolders(rows);
    } catch (err) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";
      Alert.alert("Failed to load folders", message);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    loadFolders();
  }, [accessToken]);

  async function handleCreateFolder() {
    if (!accessToken) {
      Alert.alert("Not authenticated", "Please login again.");
      return;
    }

    if (!folderName.trim()) {
      Alert.alert("Folder name required", "Enter a folder name.");
      return;
    }

    setCreating(true);
    try {
      await apiFetch<FolderRow>("/folders", accessToken, {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({
          name: folderName.trim(),
          prompt_template: "",
        }),
      });

      setFolderName("");
      setShowCreateForm(false);
      await loadFolders();
    } catch (err) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";
      Alert.alert("Create folder failed", message);
    } finally {
      setCreating(false);
    }
  }

  function openFolder(folder: FolderRow) {
    router.push({
      pathname: "/hero-folder/[folderId]" as any,
      params: {
        folderId: folder.id,
        picker: pickerMode ? "1" : "0",
      },
    } as any);
  }

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.safe}>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.container}>
        <View style={styles.headerRow}>
          <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
            <Text style={styles.title}>
              {pickerMode ? "Select Hero Folder" : "Hero Image"}
            </Text>
            <Text style={styles.subtitle}>
              {pickerMode
                ? "Choose a folder, then select a hero image"
                : "Create folders and open a folder to manage hero images"}
            </Text>
          </View>
          <Pressable style={styles.secondaryBtnSmall} onPress={() => router.back()}>
            <Text style={styles.secondaryBtnSmallText}>Back</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.card}>
          <View style={styles.actionHeader}>
            <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>Folders</Text>
            <Pressable
              style={styles.primaryBtnSmall}
              onPress={() => setShowCreateForm((prev) => !prev)}
              disabled={creating}
            >
              <Text style={styles.primaryBtnSmallText}>
                {showCreateForm ? "Close" : "Create New Folder"}
              </Text>
            </Pressable>
          </View>

          {showCreateForm ? (
            <View style={styles.createBox}>
              <Text style={styles.label}>Folder Name</Text>
              <TextInput
                style={styles.input}
                placeholder="e.g. Suit, Saree, Sherwani"
                value={folderName}
                onChangeText={setFolderName}
              />
              <Pressable
                style={[styles.primaryBtn, creating && styles.disabledBtn]}
                onPress={handleCreateFolder}
                disabled={creating}
              >
                {creating ? (
                  <ActivityIndicator color="#fff" />
                ) : (
                  <Text style={styles.primaryBtnText}>Create Folder</Text>
                )}
              </Pressable>
            </View>
          ) : null}

          <Pressable
            style={[styles.secondaryBtn, loading && styles.disabledBtn]}
            onPress={loadFolders}
            disabled={loading || creating}
          >
            <Text style={styles.secondaryBtnText}>
              {loading ? "Refreshing..." : "Refresh Folders"}
            </Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.gridWrap}>
          {loading ? (
            <View style={styles.emptyState}>
              <ActivityIndicator />
              <Text style={styles.emptyStateText}>Loading folders...</Text>
            </View>
          ) : folders.length === 0 ? (
            <View style={styles.emptyState}>
              <Text style={styles.emptyStateText}>No folders yet.</Text>
              <Text style={styles.helperText}>Create one to start adding hero images.</Text>
            </View>
          ) : (
            folders.map((folder) => (
              <Pressable
                key={folder.id}
                style={[styles.folderTile, !folder.is_active && styles.folderTileInactive]}
                onPress={() => openFolder(folder)}
              >
                <View style={styles.folderIcon}>
                  <View style={styles.folderTab} />
                  <View style={styles.folderBody} />
                </View>
                <Text style={styles.folderName} numberOfLines={2}>
                  {folder.name}
                </Text>
                {!folder.is_active ? (
                  <Text style={styles.folderBadge}>Inactive</Text>
                ) : null}
              </Pressable>
            ))
          )}
        </View>
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

import { router } from "expo-router";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
  Image,
  Pressable,
  SafeAreaView,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Switch,
  Text,
  View,
} from "react-native";

import { useSupabaseSession } from "../lib/auth";
import { apiFetch } from "../lib/backend";
import {
  buildGenerationDownloadFilename,
  downloadAndSaveImageToPhotos,
} from "../lib/downloads";

type GenerationStatus = "queued" | "processing" | "done" | "failed" | string;

type GenerationRow = {
  id: string;
  shop_id: string;
  hero_image_id: string;
  fabric_image_id: string;
  folder_id: string;
  status: GenerationStatus;
  prompt_used: string | null;
  nano_request_id: string | null;
  output_path: string | null;
  error: string | null;
  credits_used: number;
  created_at: string;
  started_at: string | null;
  completed_at: string | null;
};

type DownloadUrlResponse = {
  generation_id: string;
  download_url: string;
  expires_in_seconds: number;
};

const HISTORY_LIMIT = 50;
const AUTO_POLL_INTERVAL_MS = 4000;

function formatDateLabel(value?: string | null) {
  if (!value) return "-";
  const d = new Date(value);
  if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return value;
  return d.toLocaleString();
}

function isPendingStatus(status?: string | null) {
  return status === "queued" || status === "processing";
}

function statusBadgeStyle(status: string) {
  if (status === "done") return styles.badgeDone;
  if (status === "failed") return styles.badgeFailed;
  if (status === "processing") return styles.badgeProcessing;
  return styles.badgeQueued;
}

function truncateText(value: string | null | undefined, max = 260) {
  if (!value) return "-";
  if (value.length <= max) return value;
  return `${value.slice(0, max)}...`;
}

export default function HistoryScreen() {
  const { accessToken } = useSupabaseSession();

  const [generations, setGenerations] = useState<GenerationRow[]>([]);
  const [selectedGenerationId, setSelectedGenerationId] = useState<string>("");
  const [selectedGeneration, setSelectedGeneration] = useState<GenerationRow | null>(null);

  const [downloadUrl, setDownloadUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [downloading, setDownloading] = useState(false);
  const [downloadStatusText, setDownloadStatusText] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [loadingHistory, setLoadingHistory] = useState(false);
  const [loadingDetail, setLoadingDetail] = useState(false);
  const [autoPoll, setAutoPoll] = useState(true);

  const [statusText, setStatusText] = useState("Open history to load generations.");
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<"all" | "queued" | "processing" | "done" | "failed">("all");

  const selectedStatus = selectedGeneration?.status ?? null;

  const filteredGenerations = useMemo(() => {
    if (statusFilter === "all") return generations;
    return generations.filter((g) => g.status === statusFilter);
  }, [generations, statusFilter]);

  async function fetchGenerationDownloadUrl(token: string, generationId: string) {
    const response = await apiFetch<DownloadUrlResponse>(
      `/generations/${generationId}/download-url`,
      token,
      { method: "GET" }
    );

    return response.download_url;
  }

  async function loadHistory(token: string, silent = false) {
    if (!silent) setLoadingHistory(true);

    try {
      const query =
        statusFilter === "all"
          ? `/generations?limit=${HISTORY_LIMIT}`
          : `/generations?status=${statusFilter}&limit=${HISTORY_LIMIT}`;

      const rows = await apiFetch<GenerationRow[]>(query, token, { method: "GET" });

      setGenerations(rows);

      setSelectedGenerationId((prev) => {
        if (prev && rows.some((r) => r.id === prev)) return prev;
        return rows[0]?.id ?? "";
      });

      if (!silent) {
        setStatusText(`Loaded ${rows.length} generation(s).`);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";
      setStatusText(`History load failed: ${message}`);
    } finally {
      if (!silent) setLoadingHistory(false);
    }
  }

  async function loadDownloadUrlIfReady(token: string, generation: GenerationRow) {
    if (generation.status !== "done" || !generation.output_path) {
      setDownloadUrl(null);
      return;
    }

    try {
      const signedUrl = await fetchGenerationDownloadUrl(token, generation.id);
      setDownloadUrl(signedUrl);
    } catch (err) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";

      // If the worker just finished but row and URL aren't fully in sync yet, don't hard-fail UI.
      if (message.toLowerCase().includes("not ready")) {
        setDownloadUrl(null);
        return;
      }

      setDownloadUrl(null);
      setStatusText(`Could not load result preview: ${message}`);
    }
  }

  async function loadGenerationDetail(token: string, generationId: string, silent = false) {
    if (!generationId) {
      setSelectedGeneration(null);
      setDownloadUrl(null);
      return;
    }

    if (!silent) setLoadingDetail(true);

    try {
      const row = await apiFetch<GenerationRow>(`/generations/${generationId}`, token, {
        method: "GET",
      });

      setSelectedGeneration(row);
      if (row.status !== "done") {
        setDownloadStatusText(null);
      }
      await loadDownloadUrlIfReady(token, row);

      if (!silent) {
        setStatusText(`Loaded generation ${row.id} (${row.status}).`);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";
      setStatusText(`Detail load failed: ${message}`);
      setSelectedGeneration(null);
      setDownloadUrl(null);
    } finally {
      if (!silent) setLoadingDetail(false);
    }
  }

  async function refreshAll() {
    if (!accessToken) return;

    setStatusText("Refreshing history...");
    await loadHistory(accessToken);
  }

  async function handleDownloadToPhotos() {
    if (!accessToken || !selectedGeneration) return;

    if (selectedGeneration.status !== "done" || !selectedGeneration.output_path) {
      Alert.alert(
        "Output not ready",
        "Download is available only when generation status is done."
      );
      return;
    }

    setDownloading(true);
    setDownloadStatusText("Refreshing secure download link...");

    try {
      const freshSignedUrl = await fetchGenerationDownloadUrl(
        accessToken,
        selectedGeneration.id
      );
      setDownloadUrl(freshSignedUrl);

      const filename = buildGenerationDownloadFilename(
        selectedGeneration.id,
        selectedGeneration.output_path,
        freshSignedUrl
      );

      await downloadAndSaveImageToPhotos({
        url: freshSignedUrl,
        filename,
        onStatusChange: (message) => setDownloadStatusText(message),
      });

      setDownloadStatusText("Saved to Photos");
      Alert.alert("Download complete", "Generated image saved to Photos.");
    } catch (err) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";
      setDownloadStatusText(`Download failed: ${message}`);
      Alert.alert("Download failed", message);
    } finally {
      setDownloading(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!accessToken) {
      setGenerations([]);
      setSelectedGenerationId("");
      setSelectedGeneration(null);
      setDownloadUrl(null);
      setDownloadStatusText(null);
      return;
    }

    loadHistory(accessToken);
  }, [accessToken, statusFilter]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!accessToken || !selectedGenerationId) return;
    loadGenerationDetail(accessToken, selectedGenerationId);
  }, [accessToken, selectedGenerationId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!accessToken || !selectedGenerationId || !autoPoll) return;
    if (!isPendingStatus(selectedStatus)) return;

    const timer = setInterval(async () => {
      try {
        await loadGenerationDetail(accessToken, selectedGenerationId, true);
        await loadHistory(accessToken, true);
      } catch {
        // UI state already handled inside loaders
      }
    }, AUTO_POLL_INTERVAL_MS);

    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [accessToken, selectedGenerationId, selectedStatus, autoPoll, statusFilter]);

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.safe}>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.container}>
        <View style={styles.headerRow}>
          <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
            <Text style={styles.title}>Generation History</Text>
            <Text style={styles.muted}>
              Status polling + result preview (Step 29)
            </Text>
          </View>
          <Pressable style={styles.secondaryBtnSmall} onPress={() => router.back()}>
            <Text style={styles.secondaryBtnText}>Back</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.card}>
          <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>Controls</Text>

          <Text style={styles.muted}>{statusText}</Text>

          <View style={styles.switchRow}>
            <Text style={styles.label}>Auto Poll Pending Generation</Text>
            <Switch value={autoPoll} onValueChange={setAutoPoll} />
          </View>

          <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Poll Interval</Text>
          <Text style={styles.muted}>{AUTO_POLL_INTERVAL_MS / 1000}s</Text>

          <View style={styles.filterWrap}>
            {(["all", "queued", "processing", "done", "failed"] as const).map((value) => {
              const selected = statusFilter === value;
              return (
                <Pressable
                  key={value}
                  style={[styles.filterChip, selected && styles.filterChipSelected]}
                  onPress={() => setStatusFilter(value)}
                >
                  <Text
                    style={[
                      styles.filterChipText,
                      selected && styles.filterChipTextSelected,
                    ]}
                  >
                    {value}
                  </Text>
                </Pressable>
              );
            })}
          </View>

          <Pressable
            style={[styles.secondaryBtn, (loadingHistory || loadingDetail) && styles.disabledBtn]}
            onPress={refreshAll}
            disabled={loadingHistory || loadingDetail}
          >
            <Text style={styles.secondaryBtnText}>
              {loadingHistory ? "Refreshing..." : "Refresh History"}
            </Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.card}>
          <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>
            History ({filteredGenerations.length})
          </Text>

          {loadingHistory ? (
            <View style={styles.centerRow}>
              <ActivityIndicator />
              <Text style={styles.muted}>Loading history...</Text>
            </View>
          ) : filteredGenerations.length === 0 ? (
            <Text style={styles.muted}>
              No generations found for this filter.
            </Text>
          ) : (
            <View style={styles.list}>
              {filteredGenerations.map((row) => {
                const selected = row.id === selectedGenerationId;
                return (
                  <Pressable
                    key={row.id}
                    style={[styles.itemCard, selected && styles.itemCardSelected]}
                    onPress={() => setSelectedGenerationId(row.id)}
                  >
                    <View style={styles.itemTopRow}>
                      <Text style={styles.itemTitle} numberOfLines={1}>
                        {row.id}
                      </Text>
                      <View style={[styles.badge, statusBadgeStyle(row.status)]}>
                        <Text style={styles.badgeText}>{row.status}</Text>
                      </View>
                    </View>

                    <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Created</Text>
                    <Text style={styles.muted}>{formatDateLabel(row.created_at)}</Text>

                    <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Credits Used</Text>
                    <Text style={styles.muted}>{String(row.credits_used)}</Text>

                    <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Output Path</Text>
                    <Text style={styles.codeText}>{row.output_path ?? "-"}</Text>
                  </Pressable>
                );
              })}
            </View>
          )}
        </View>

        <View style={styles.card}>
          <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>Selected Generation Detail</Text>

          {!selectedGenerationId ? (
            <Text style={styles.muted}>Select a generation from the history list.</Text>
          ) : loadingDetail ? (
            <View style={styles.centerRow}>
              <ActivityIndicator />
              <Text style={styles.muted}>Loading detail...</Text>
            </View>
          ) : !selectedGeneration ? (
            <Text style={styles.muted}>No detail loaded.</Text>
          ) : (
            <View style={styles.detailBox}>
              <View style={styles.itemTopRow}>
                <Text style={styles.itemTitle} numberOfLines={1}>
                  {selectedGeneration.id}
                </Text>
                <View style={[styles.badge, statusBadgeStyle(selectedGeneration.status)]}>
                  <Text style={styles.badgeText}>{selectedGeneration.status}</Text>
                </View>
              </View>

              <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Hero Image ID</Text>
              <Text style={styles.codeText}>{selectedGeneration.hero_image_id}</Text>

              <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Fabric Image ID</Text>
              <Text style={styles.codeText}>{selectedGeneration.fabric_image_id}</Text>

              <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Folder ID</Text>
              <Text style={styles.codeText}>{selectedGeneration.folder_id}</Text>

              <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Created / Started / Completed</Text>
              <Text style={styles.muted}>
                {formatDateLabel(selectedGeneration.created_at)}
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.muted}>
                {formatDateLabel(selectedGeneration.started_at)}
              </Text>
              <Text style={styles.muted}>
                {formatDateLabel(selectedGeneration.completed_at)}
              </Text>

              <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Credits Used</Text>
              <Text style={styles.muted}>{String(selectedGeneration.credits_used)}</Text>

              <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Prompt (preview)</Text>
              <Text style={styles.promptPreview}>
                {truncateText(selectedGeneration.prompt_used, 500)}
              </Text>

              <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Output Path</Text>
              <Text style={styles.codeText}>{selectedGeneration.output_path ?? "-"}</Text>

              <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Error</Text>
              <Text style={selectedGeneration.error ? styles.errorText : styles.muted}>
                {selectedGeneration.error ?? "-"}
              </Text>

              <Pressable
                style={[styles.secondaryBtnSmall, styles.detailRefreshBtn]}
                onPress={() =>
                  accessToken && loadGenerationDetail(accessToken, selectedGeneration.id)
                }
              >
                <Text style={styles.secondaryBtnText}>Refresh Selected</Text>
              </Pressable>

              {selectedGeneration.status === "done" ? (
                <View style={styles.resultBox}>
                  <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>Result Preview</Text>
                  {downloadUrl ? (
                    <>
                      <Image source={{ uri: downloadUrl }} style={styles.preview} />
                      <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Signed URL loaded</Text>
                      <Text style={styles.codeText} numberOfLines={2}>
                        {downloadUrl}
                      </Text>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <Text style={styles.muted}>
                      Output is done but preview URL not loaded yet. Tap Refresh Selected.
                    </Text>
                  )}

                  <Pressable
                    style={[
                      styles.downloadBtn,
                      downloading && styles.disabledBtn,
                    ]}
                    onPress={handleDownloadToPhotos}
                    disabled={downloading || loadingDetail}
                  >
                    <Text style={styles.downloadBtnText}>
                      {downloading ? "Saving..." : "Download to Photos"}
                    </Text>
                  </Pressable>

                  {downloadStatusText ? (
                    <Text
                      style={
                        downloadStatusText.startsWith("Saved")
                          ? styles.successText
                          : styles.muted
                      }
                    >
                      {downloadStatusText}
                    </Text>
                  ) : null}
                </View>
              ) : isPendingStatus(selectedGeneration.status) ? (
                <Text style={styles.muted}>
                  Waiting for worker to complete this generation...
                </Text>
              ) : null}
            </View>
          )}
        </View>
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}
