import { decode } from "base64-arraybuffer";
import * as FileSystem from "expo-file-system/legacy";
import * as ImagePicker from "expo-image-picker";
import { router } from "expo-router";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
  Image,
  Pressable,
  SafeAreaView,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  View,
} from "react-native";

import { useSupabaseSession } from "../lib/auth";
import { apiFetch } from "../lib/backend";
import { supabase } from "../lib/supabase";

type UploadMode = "hero" | "fabric";
type InputSource = "gallery" | "camera";

type ShopContext = {
  auth_user_id: string;
  email: string | null;
  shop_id: string;
  role: string;
};

type FolderRow = {
  id: string;
  shop_id: string;
  name: string;
  prompt_template: string;
  is_active: boolean;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
};

type HeroImageRow = {
  id: string;
  shop_id: string;
  folder_id: string;
  storage_path: string;
  original_filename: string | null;
  mime_type: string | null;
  file_size_bytes: number | null;
  width: number | null;
  height: number | null;
  created_at: string;
};

type FabricImageRow = {
  id: string;
  shop_id: string;
  storage_path: string;
  original_filename: string | null;
  mime_type: string | null;
  file_size_bytes: number | null;
  width: number | null;
  height: number | null;
  created_at: string;
};

type PreviewUrlMap = Record<string, string>;

const IMAGE_PREVIEW_SIGNED_URL_TTL_SECONDS = 3600;

function makeRandomSuffix() {
  return Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8);
}

function isHeroImageRow(row: HeroImageRow | FabricImageRow): row is HeroImageRow {
  return "folder_id" in row;
}

function guessFileExtension(asset: ImagePicker.ImagePickerAsset): string {
  const fromName = asset.fileName?.split(".").pop()?.toLowerCase();
  const fromMime = asset.mimeType?.split("/").pop()?.toLowerCase();
  const raw = fromName || fromMime || "jpg";
  return raw.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, "") || "jpg";
}

function formatDateLabel(value?: string | null) {
  if (!value) return "-";
  const d = new Date(value);
  if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return value;
  return d.toLocaleString();
}

function getImageBucketForMode(mode: UploadMode) {
  return mode === "hero" ? "hero-images" : "fabric-images";
}

function getPreviewCacheKey(bucket: string, rowId: string) {
  return `${bucket}:${rowId}`;
}

export default function ImagesScreen() {
  const { accessToken } = useSupabaseSession();

  const [shopContext, setShopContext] = useState<ShopContext | null>(null);
  const [folders, setFolders] = useState<FolderRow[]>([]);
  const [selectedFolderId, setSelectedFolderId] = useState<string>("");

  const [heroImages, setHeroImages] = useState<HeroImageRow[]>([]);
  const [fabricImages, setFabricImages] = useState<FabricImageRow[]>([]);

  const [mode, setMode] = useState<UploadMode>("hero");

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);

  const [lastPreviewUri, setLastPreviewUri] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [lastStoragePath, setLastStoragePath] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [lastStatus, setLastStatus] = useState<string>("No uploads yet");
  const [previewUrlsByKey, setPreviewUrlsByKey] = useState<PreviewUrlMap>({});

  const selectedFolder = useMemo(
    () => folders.find((f) => f.id === selectedFolderId) ?? null,
    [folders, selectedFolderId]
  );

  const visibleRows = mode === "hero" ? heroImages : fabricImages;

  async function getStorageSignedPreviewUrl(bucket: string, storagePath: string) {
    const { data, error } = await supabase.storage
      .from(bucket)
      .createSignedUrl(storagePath, IMAGE_PREVIEW_SIGNED_URL_TTL_SECONDS);

    if (error) {
      throw new Error(error.message);
    }

    const signedUrl =
      (data as { signedUrl?: string; signedURL?: string } | null)?.signedUrl ||
      (data as { signedUrl?: string; signedURL?: string } | null)?.signedURL ||
      null;

    if (!signedUrl) {
      throw new Error("Signed URL response was empty");
    }

    return signedUrl;
  }

  async function loadVisibleImagePreviews(rows: Array<HeroImageRow | FabricImageRow>) {
    const bucket = getImageBucketForMode(mode);

    const rowsNeedingPreview = rows.filter((row) => {
      const key = getPreviewCacheKey(bucket, row.id);
      return !previewUrlsByKey[key];
    });

    if (rowsNeedingPreview.length === 0) return;

    const results = await Promise.all(
      rowsNeedingPreview.map(async (row) => {
        try {
          const signedUrl = await getStorageSignedPreviewUrl(bucket, row.storage_path);
          return {
            key: getPreviewCacheKey(bucket, row.id),
            signedUrl,
          };
        } catch {
          return null;
        }
      })
    );

    const entries = results.filter(
      (item): item is { key: string; signedUrl: string } => !!item
    );

    if (entries.length === 0) return;

    setPreviewUrlsByKey((prev) => {
      const next = { ...prev };
      for (const entry of entries) {
        next[entry.key] = entry.signedUrl;
      }
      return next;
    });
  }

  async function loadShopContext(token: string): Promise<ShopContext> {
    const me = await apiFetch<ShopContext>("/me", token, { method: "GET" });
    setShopContext(me);
    return me;
  }

  async function loadFolders(token: string): Promise<FolderRow[]> {
    const rows = await apiFetch<FolderRow[]>("/folders?include_inactive=true", token, {
      method: "GET",
    });
    setFolders(rows);

    setSelectedFolderId((prev) => {
      if (prev && rows.some((r) => r.id === prev)) return prev;
      const firstActive = rows.find((r) => r.is_active);
      return firstActive?.id ?? rows[0]?.id ?? "";
    });

    return rows;
  }

  async function loadHeroImages(token: string, folderId?: string) {
    const query = folderId ? `?folder_id=${encodeURIComponent(folderId)}` : "";
    const rows = await apiFetch<HeroImageRow[]>(`/hero-images${query}`, token, {
      method: "GET",
    });
    setHeroImages(rows);
  }

  async function loadFabricImages(token: string) {
    const rows = await apiFetch<FabricImageRow[]>("/fabric-images", token, {
      method: "GET",
    });
    setFabricImages(rows);
  }

  async function refreshAll() {
    if (!accessToken) return;

    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const me = await loadShopContext(accessToken);
      const folderRows = await loadFolders(accessToken);

      const folderIdForHero =
        (selectedFolderId && folderRows.some((f) => f.id === selectedFolderId)
          ? selectedFolderId
          : folderRows.find((f) => f.is_active)?.id || folderRows[0]?.id || "") || "";

      await Promise.all([
        loadHeroImages(accessToken, folderIdForHero || undefined),
        loadFabricImages(accessToken),
      ]);

      setLastStatus(`Loaded data for shop ${me.shop_id}`);
    } catch (err) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";
      setLastStatus(`Refresh failed: ${message}`);
      Alert.alert("Failed to load data", message);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!accessToken) {
      setShopContext(null);
      setFolders([]);
      setHeroImages([]);
      setFabricImages([]);
      setPreviewUrlsByKey({});
      return;
    }
    refreshAll();
  }, [accessToken]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!accessToken || !selectedFolderId) return;
    loadHeroImages(accessToken, selectedFolderId).catch((err) => {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";
      setLastStatus(`Failed to load hero images: ${message}`);
    });
  }, [accessToken, selectedFolderId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!accessToken || visibleRows.length === 0) return;

    loadVisibleImagePreviews(visibleRows).catch((err) => {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";
      setLastStatus(`Preview load failed: ${message}`);
    });
  }, [accessToken, mode, visibleRows]);

  async function ensurePermission(source: InputSource) {
    if (source === "gallery") {
      const res = await ImagePicker.requestMediaLibraryPermissionsAsync();
      if (!res.granted) {
        throw new Error("Media library permission not granted");
      }
      return;
    }

    const res = await ImagePicker.requestCameraPermissionsAsync();
    if (!res.granted) {
      throw new Error("Camera permission not granted");
    }
  }

  async function pickAsset(source: InputSource) {
    if (source === "camera") {
      return ImagePicker.launchCameraAsync({
        mediaTypes: ImagePicker.MediaTypeOptions.Images,
        quality: 1,
      });
    }

    return ImagePicker.launchImageLibraryAsync({
      mediaTypes: ImagePicker.MediaTypeOptions.Images,
      quality: 1,
    });
  }

  async function handleUpload(source: InputSource) {
    if (!accessToken) {
      Alert.alert("Not authenticated", "Please login again.");
      return;
    }

    if (!shopContext) {
      Alert.alert("Not ready", "Shop context is not loaded yet.");
      return;
    }

    if (mode === "hero" && !selectedFolderId) {
      Alert.alert("Folder required", "Select a folder before uploading a hero image.");
      return;
    }

    setUploading(true);
    try {
      setLastStatus(`Requesting ${source} permission...`);
      await ensurePermission(source);

      setLastStatus(source === "camera" ? "Opening camera..." : "Opening gallery...");
      const pickerResult = await pickAsset(source);

      if (pickerResult.canceled) {
        setLastStatus("Image selection canceled.");
        return;
      }

      const asset = pickerResult.assets[0];
      if (!asset?.uri) {
        throw new Error("No image selected");
      }

      setLastPreviewUri(asset.uri);

      const ext = guessFileExtension(asset);
      const filename = `${Date.now()}-${makeRandomSuffix()}.${ext}`;

      const bucket = mode === "hero" ? "hero-images" : "fabric-images";
      const storagePath =
        mode === "hero"
          ? `${shopContext.shop_id}/${selectedFolderId}/${filename}`
          : `${shopContext.shop_id}/${filename}`;

      setLastStatus("Reading selected image...");
      const base64 = await FileSystem.readAsStringAsync(asset.uri, {
        encoding: "base64",
      });

      setLastStatus(`Uploading to ${bucket}...`);
      const { error: uploadError } = await supabase.storage
        .from(bucket)
        .upload(storagePath, decode(base64), {
          contentType: asset.mimeType || "image/jpeg",
          upsert: false,
        });

      if (uploadError) {
        throw new Error(uploadError.message);
      }

      setLastStoragePath(storagePath);

      setLastStatus("Saving image metadata to backend...");
      if (mode === "hero") {
        await apiFetch<HeroImageRow>("/hero-images", accessToken, {
          method: "POST",
          body: JSON.stringify({
            folder_id: selectedFolderId,
            storage_path: storagePath,
            original_filename: asset.fileName ?? filename,
            mime_type: asset.mimeType ?? "image/jpeg",
            file_size_bytes: asset.fileSize ?? null,
            width: asset.width ?? null,
            height: asset.height ?? null,
          }),
        });
      } else {
        await apiFetch<FabricImageRow>("/fabric-images", accessToken, {
          method: "POST",
          body: JSON.stringify({
            storage_path: storagePath,
            original_filename: asset.fileName ?? filename,
            mime_type: asset.mimeType ?? "image/jpeg",
            file_size_bytes: asset.fileSize ?? null,
            width: asset.width ?? null,
            height: asset.height ?? null,
          }),
        });
      }

      await Promise.all([
        loadHeroImages(accessToken, selectedFolderId || undefined),
        loadFabricImages(accessToken),
      ]);

      setLastStatus(
        `${mode === "hero" ? "Hero" : "Fabric"} upload successful (${source}).`
      );
      Alert.alert("Success", "Image uploaded and metadata saved.");
    } catch (err) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";
      setLastStatus(`Upload failed: ${message}`);
      Alert.alert("Upload failed", message);
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.safe}>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.container}>
        <View style={styles.headerRow}>
          <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
            <Text style={styles.title}>Images</Text>
            <Text style={styles.muted}>
              Upload hero/fabric images via gallery or camera
            </Text>
          </View>
          <Pressable style={styles.secondaryBtnSmall} onPress={() => router.back()}>
            <Text style={styles.secondaryBtnText}>Back</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.card}>
          <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>Context</Text>
          <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Shop ID</Text>
          <Text style={styles.codeText}>{shopContext?.shop_id ?? "-"}</Text>
          <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Status</Text>
          <Text style={styles.muted}>{lastStatus}</Text>

          <Pressable
            style={[styles.secondaryBtn, (loading || uploading) && styles.disabledBtn]}
            onPress={refreshAll}
            disabled={loading || uploading}
          >
            <Text style={styles.secondaryBtnText}>
              {loading ? "Refreshing..." : "Refresh Data"}
            </Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.card}>
          <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>Upload Mode</Text>

          <View style={styles.row}>
            <Pressable
              style={[styles.modeBtn, mode === "hero" && styles.modeBtnActive]}
              onPress={() => setMode("hero")}
              disabled={uploading}
            >
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.modeBtnText,
                  mode === "hero" && styles.modeBtnTextActive,
                ]}
              >
                Hero
              </Text>
            </Pressable>

            <Pressable
              style={[styles.modeBtn, mode === "fabric" && styles.modeBtnActive]}
              onPress={() => setMode("fabric")}
              disabled={uploading}
            >
              <Text
                style={[
                  styles.modeBtnText,
                  mode === "fabric" && styles.modeBtnTextActive,
                ]}
              >
                Fabric
              </Text>
            </Pressable>
          </View>

          {mode === "hero" ? (
            <>
              <Text style={styles.label}>Select Folder (required for hero)</Text>

              {folders.length === 0 ? (
                <Text style={styles.muted}>
                  No folders found. Create a folder first from Folder Management.
                </Text>
              ) : (
                <View style={styles.chipsWrap}>
                  {folders.map((folder) => {
                    const selected = folder.id === selectedFolderId;
                    return (
                      <Pressable
                        key={folder.id}
                        style={[
                          styles.chip,
                          selected && styles.chipSelected,
                          !folder.is_active && styles.chipInactive,
                        ]}
                        onPress={() => setSelectedFolderId(folder.id)}
                        disabled={uploading}
                      >
                        <Text
                          style={[
                            styles.chipText,
                            selected && styles.chipTextSelected,
                          ]}
                        >
                          {folder.name}
                          {folder.is_active ? "" : " (inactive)"}
                        </Text>
                      </Pressable>
                    );
                  })}
                </View>
              )}

              <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Selected Folder ID</Text>
              <Text style={styles.codeText}>{selectedFolderId || "-"}</Text>
              <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Selected Folder Name</Text>
              <Text style={styles.muted}>{selectedFolder?.name ?? "-"}</Text>
            </>
          ) : null}

          <View style={styles.buttonRow}>
            <Pressable
              style={[styles.primaryBtn, uploading && styles.disabledBtn]}
              onPress={() => handleUpload("gallery")}
              disabled={uploading || loading || (mode === "hero" && !selectedFolderId)}
            >
              <Text style={styles.primaryBtnText}>
                {uploading ? "Working..." : "Upload from Gallery"}
              </Text>
            </Pressable>

            <Pressable
              style={[styles.primaryBtnAlt, uploading && styles.disabledBtn]}
              onPress={() => handleUpload("camera")}
              disabled={uploading || loading || (mode === "hero" && !selectedFolderId)}
            >
              <Text style={styles.primaryBtnAltText}>
                {uploading ? "Working..." : "Capture from Camera"}
              </Text>
            </Pressable>
          </View>
        </View>

        {lastPreviewUri ? (
          <View style={styles.card}>
            <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>Last Selected Image</Text>
            <Image source={{ uri: lastPreviewUri }} style={styles.preview} />
            <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Last Storage Path</Text>
            <Text style={styles.codeText}>{lastStoragePath ?? "-"}</Text>
          </View>
        ) : null}

        <View style={styles.card}>
          <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>
            {mode === "hero" ? "Recent Hero Images" : "Recent Fabric Images"}
          </Text>

          {loading ? (
            <View style={styles.centerRow}>
              <ActivityIndicator />
              <Text style={styles.muted}>Loading...</Text>
            </View>
          ) : visibleRows.length === 0 ? (
            <Text style={styles.muted}>No images found for this list.</Text>
          ) : (
            <View style={styles.list}>
              {visibleRows.map((row) => (
                <View key={row.id} style={styles.itemCard}>
                  <Text style={styles.itemTitle}>
                    {row.original_filename || "Unnamed image"}
                  </Text>

                  {(() => {
                    const bucket = getImageBucketForMode(mode);
                    const previewUrl =
                      previewUrlsByKey[getPreviewCacheKey(bucket, row.id)] ?? null;

                    return previewUrl ? (
                      <Image
                        source={{ uri: previewUrl }}
                        style={styles.listPreview}
                        resizeMode="cover"
                      />
                    ) : (
                      <View style={styles.listPreviewPlaceholder}>
                        <Text style={styles.listPreviewPlaceholderText}>
                          Preview loading...
                        </Text>
                      </View>
                    );
                  })()}

                  <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Image ID</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.codeText}>{row.id}</Text>

                  {isHeroImageRow(row) ? (
                    <>
                      <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Folder ID</Text>
                      <Text style={styles.codeText}>{row.folder_id}</Text>
                    </>
                  ) : null}

                  <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Storage Path</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.codeText}>{row.storage_path}</Text>

                  <Text style={styles.metaLabel}>Created</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.muted}>{formatDateLabel(row.created_at)}</Text>
                </View>
              ))}
            </View>
          )}
        </View>
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({

import { router } from "expo-router";
import { useState } from "react";
import {
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
  Pressable,
  SafeAreaView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  TextInput,
  View,
} from "react-native";

import { supabase } from "../lib/supabase";

type Mode = "login" | "signup";

export default function LoginScreen() {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<Mode>("login");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  const [shopName, setShopName] = useState("");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("yashrami77@gmail.com");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");

  async function handleSubmit() {
    if (!email.trim() || !password) {
      Alert.alert("Missing fields", "Email and password are required.");
      return;
    }

    if (mode === "signup" && !shopName.trim()) {
      Alert.alert("Missing shop name", "Shop name is required for signup.");
      return;
    }

    setBusy(true);
    try {
      if (mode === "login") {
        const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({
          email: email.trim(),
          password,
        });

        if (error) throw error;

        router.replace("/(tabs)");
        return;
      }

      const { error } = await supabase.auth.signUp({
        email: email.trim(),
        password,
        options: {
          data: {
            shop_name: shopName.trim(),
          },
        },
      });

      if (error) throw error;

      Alert.alert(
        "Signup successful",
        "Account created. You can now continue into the app."
      );
      router.replace("/(tabs)");
    } catch (err) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";
      Alert.alert("Auth failed", message);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.safe}>
      <View style={styles.container}>
        <Text style={styles.title}>Ai Vastra</Text>
        <Text style={styles.subtitle}>Shop account access</Text>

        <View style={styles.modeRow}>
          <Pressable
            style={[styles.modeButton, mode === "login" && styles.modeButtonActive]}
            onPress={() => setMode("login")}
            disabled={busy}
          >
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.modeButtonText,
                mode === "login" && styles.modeButtonTextActive,
              ]}
            >
              Login
            </Text>
          </Pressable>

          <Pressable
            style={[styles.modeButton, mode === "signup" && styles.modeButtonActive]}
            onPress={() => setMode("signup")}
            disabled={busy}
          >
            <Text
              style={[
                styles.modeButtonText,
                mode === "signup" && styles.modeButtonTextActive,
              ]}
            >
              Signup
            </Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>

        {mode === "signup" ? (
          <>
            <Text style={styles.label}>Shop Name</Text>
            <TextInput
              style={styles.input}
              value={shopName}
              onChangeText={setShopName}
              placeholder="e.g. Yash Tailors"
            />
          </>
        ) : null}

        <Text style={styles.label}>Email</Text>
        <TextInput
          style={styles.input}
          value={email}
          onChangeText={setEmail}
          autoCapitalize="none"
          keyboardType="email-address"
          placeholder="shop@example.com"
        />

        <Text style={styles.label}>Password</Text>
        <TextInput
          style={styles.input}
          value={password}
          onChangeText={setPassword}
          secureTextEntry
          placeholder="Enter password"
        />

        <Pressable
          style={[styles.primaryButton, busy && styles.disabledButton]}
          onPress={handleSubmit}
          disabled={busy}
        >
          {busy ? (
            <ActivityIndicator color="#fff" />
          ) : (
            <Text style={styles.primaryButtonText}>
              {mode === "login" ? "Login" : "Create Shop Account"}
            </Text>
          )}
        </Pressable>

        <Text style={styles.helpText}>
          {mode === "login"
            ? "Use the shop account email/password."
            : "Signup creates a shop automatically (via Supabase DB trigger)."}
        </Text>
      </View>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  safe: {
    flex: 1,
    backgroundColor: "#f5f3ee",
  },
  container: {
    flex: 1,
    padding: 20,
    justifyContent: "center",
    gap: 10,
  },
  title: {
    fontSize: 30,
    fontWeight: "700",
    color: "#1f2937",
  },
  subtitle: {
    color: "#6b7280",
    marginBottom: 12,
  },
  modeRow: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    backgroundColor: "#e7e0d2",
    borderRadius: 10,
    padding: 4,
    marginBottom: 10,
  },
  modeButton: {
    flex: 1,
    paddingVertical: 10,
    borderRadius: 8,
    alignItems: "center",
  },
  modeButtonActive: {
    backgroundColor: "#4f46e5",
  },
  modeButtonText: {
    fontWeight: "600",
    color: "#4b5563",
  },
  modeButtonTextActive: {
    color: "#fff",
  },
  label: {
    fontWeight: "600",
    color: "#374151",
    marginTop: 4,
  },
  input: {
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: "#d1d5db",
    backgroundColor: "#fff",
    borderRadius: 10,
    paddingHorizontal: 12,
    paddingVertical: 12,
  },
  primaryButton: {
    backgroundColor: "#111827",
    borderRadius: 10,
    paddingVertical: 14,
    alignItems: "center",
    marginTop: 12,
  },
  disabledButton: {
    opacity: 0.7,
  },
  primaryButtonText: {
    color: "#fff",
    fontWeight: "700",
  },
  helpText: {
    marginTop: 6,
    color: "#6b7280",
    fontSize: 12,
  },
});

=== D:\AiVastraReactJSDemo 2.2 Codex\references\mobile-backend\mobile\app\match-color.tsx ===
import Slider from "@react-native-community/slider";
import {
  Canvas,
  Fill,
  Image as SkiaImage,
  ImageShader,
  Shader,
  Skia,
  useImage,
} from "@shopify/react-native-skia";
import * as FileSystem from "expo-file-system/legacy";
import { router, useLocalSearchParams } from "expo-router";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import {
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
  Image,
  Pressable,
  SafeAreaView,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  View,
  useWindowDimensions,
} from "react-native";
import ImageColors from "react-native-image-colors";

import { useSupabaseSession } from "../lib/auth";
import { apiFetch } from "../lib/backend";

type GenerationRow = {
  id: string;
  status: string;
  output_path: string | null;
  error: string | null;
};

type DownloadUrlResponse = {
  generation_id: string;
  download_url: string;
  expires_in_seconds: number;
};

type MatchColorSaveResponse = {
  generation_id: string;
  output_path: string;
  edited: boolean;
  applied_edits?: number;
};

type SwatchAdjustment = {
  hueShiftDeg: number;
  satDeltaPct: number;
  lightDeltaPct: number;
};

const HUE_TOL = 18 / 360;
const SAT_TOL = 0.28;
const LIGHT_TOL = 0.28;
const FEATHER = 0.08;

const MATCH_COLOR_SHADER_SOURCE = `
uniform shader image;
uniform vec3 targetHsl;
uniform float hueShift;
uniform float satDelta;
uniform float lightDelta;
uniform float hueTol;
uniform float satTol;
uniform float lightTol;
uniform float feather;

float hueDistance(float a, float b) {
  float d = abs(a - b);
  return min(d, 1.0 - d);
}

vec3 rgb2hsl(vec3 c) {
  float maxC = max(c.r, max(c.g, c.b));
  float minC = min(c.r, min(c.g, c.b));
  float delta = maxC - minC;

  float h = 0.0;
  float s = 0.0;
  float l = (maxC + minC) * 0.5;

  if (delta > 0.00001) {
    if (maxC == c.r) {
      h = mod(((c.g - c.b) / delta), 6.0);
    } else if (maxC == c.g) {
      h = ((c.b - c.r) / delta) + 2.0;
    } else {
      h = ((c.r - c.g) / delta) + 4.0;
    }
    h = h / 6.0;
    if (h < 0.0) {
      h += 1.0;
    }

    s = delta / (1.0 - abs(2.0 * l - 1.0));
  }

  return vec3(h, clamp(s, 0.0, 1.0), clamp(l, 0.0, 1.0));
}

float hue2rgb(float p, float q, float t) {
  if (t < 0.0) t += 1.0;
  if (t > 1.0) t -= 1.0;
  if (t < 1.0 / 6.0) return p + (q - p) * 6.0 * t;
  if (t < 1.0 / 2.0) return q;
  if (t < 2.0 / 3.0) return p + (q - p) * (2.0 / 3.0 - t) * 6.0;
  return p;
}

vec3 hsl2rgb(vec3 hsl) {
  float h = hsl.x;
  float s = clamp(hsl.y, 0.0, 1.0);
  float l = clamp(hsl.z, 0.0, 1.0);

  if (s <= 0.00001) {
    return vec3(l, l, l);
  }

  float q = l < 0.5 ? l * (1.0 + s) : l + s - l * s;
  float p = 2.0 * l - q;

  return vec3(
    hue2rgb(p, q, h + 1.0 / 3.0),
    hue2rgb(p, q, h),
    hue2rgb(p, q, h - 1.0 / 3.0)
  );
}

half4 main(float2 xy) {
  half4 src = image.eval(xy);
  vec3 srcRgb = vec3(src.r, src.g, src.b);
  vec3 hsl = rgb2hsl(srcRgb);

  float hueDiff = hueDistance(hsl.x, targetHsl.x);
  float satDiff = abs(hsl.y - targetHsl.y);
  float lightDiff = abs(hsl.z - targetHsl.z);

  float hMask = 1.0 - smoothstep(hueTol, hueTol + feather, hueDiff);
  float sMask = 1.0 - smoothstep(satTol, satTol + feather, satDiff);
  float lMask = 1.0 - smoothstep(lightTol, lightTol + feather, lightDiff);
  float mask = clamp(hMask * sMask * lMask, 0.0, 1.0);

  vec3 shifted = hsl;
  shifted.x = fract(shifted.x + hueShift * mask + 1.0);
  shifted.y = clamp(shifted.y + satDelta * mask, 0.0, 1.0);
  shifted.z = clamp(shifted.z + lightDelta * mask, 0.0, 1.0);

  vec3 outRgb = mix(srcRgb, hsl2rgb(shifted), mask);
  return half4(outRgb.r, outRgb.g, outRgb.b, src.a);
}
`;

const MATCH_COLOR_EFFECT = Skia.RuntimeEffect.Make(MATCH_COLOR_SHADER_SOURCE);

function emptySwatchAdjustment(): SwatchAdjustment {
  return {
    hueShiftDeg: 0,
    satDeltaPct: 0,
    lightDeltaPct: 0,
  };
}

function isZeroAdjustment(value: SwatchAdjustment | null | undefined) {
  if (!value) return true;
  return (
    value.hueShiftDeg === 0 &&
    value.satDeltaPct === 0 &&
    value.lightDeltaPct === 0
  );
}

function clamp(value: number, min: number, max: number) {
  return Math.min(max, Math.max(min, value));
}

function normalizeHex(value: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  if (!value || typeof value !== "string") return null;
  const raw = value.trim();
  if (!raw.startsWith("#")) return null;
  const hex = raw.slice(1);

  if (/^[0-9a-fA-F]{3}$/.test(hex)) {
    const [r, g, b] = hex.split("");
    return `#${r}${r}${g}${g}${b}${b}`.toUpperCase();
  }

  if (/^[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(hex)) {
    return `#${hex}`.toUpperCase();
  }

  if (/^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}$/.test(hex)) {
    return `#${hex.slice(2)}`.toUpperCase(); // drop alpha
  }

  return null;
}

function hexToRgb(hex: string) {
  const normalized = normalizeHex(hex);
  if (!normalized) return null;
  const raw = normalized.slice(1);
  return {
    r: parseInt(raw.slice(0, 2), 16),
    g: parseInt(raw.slice(2, 4), 16),
    b: parseInt(raw.slice(4, 6), 16),
  };
}

function rgbToHsl01(rgb: { r: number; g: number; b: number }) {
  const r = clamp(rgb.r / 255, 0, 1);
  const g = clamp(rgb.g / 255, 0, 1);
  const b = clamp(rgb.b / 255, 0, 1);
  const max = Math.max(r, g, b);
  const min = Math.min(r, g, b);
  const delta = max - min;

  let h = 0;
  const l = (max + min) / 2;
  let s = 0;

  if (delta > 0) {
    s = delta / (1 - Math.abs(2 * l - 1));
    if (max === r) {
      h = ((g - b) / delta) % 6;
    } else if (max === g) {
      h = (b - r) / delta + 2;
    } else {
      h = (r - g) / delta + 4;
    }
    h /= 6;
    if (h < 0) h += 1;
  }

  return {
    h,
    s: Number.isFinite(s) ? clamp(s, 0, 1) : 0,
    l: clamp(l, 0, 1),
  };
}

function colorDistance(a: { r: number; g: number; b: number }, b: { r: number; g: number; b: number }) {
  const dr = a.r - b.r;
  const dg = a.g - b.g;
  const db = a.b - b.b;
  return Math.sqrt(dr * dr + dg * dg + db * db);
}

function uniqueSwatches(input: string[]) {
  const result: string[] = [];
  const seen: { hex: string; rgb: { r: number; g: number; b: number } }[] = [];

  for (const candidate of input) {
    const normalized = normalizeHex(candidate);
    if (!normalized) continue;
    const rgb = hexToRgb(normalized);
    if (!rgb) continue;

    const nearlyDuplicate = seen.some((item) => colorDistance(item.rgb, rgb) < 30);
    if (nearlyDuplicate) continue;

    seen.push({ hex: normalized, rgb });
    result.push(normalized);
  }

  return result;
}

function extractSwatches(colorsResult: any): string[] {
  const candidateKeys = [
    "dominant",
    "average",
    "vibrant",
    "lightVibrant",
    "darkVibrant",
    "muted",
    "lightMuted",
    "darkMuted",
    "primary",
    "secondary",
    "background",
    "detail",
  ];

  const candidates: string[] = [];

  for (const key of candidateKeys) {
    const value = colorsResult?.[key];
    if (typeof value === "string") candidates.push(value);
  }

  // Also scan the object for any hex-like string values, because the package shape
  // differs by platform/version.
  if (colorsResult && typeof colorsResult === "object") {
    for (const value of Object.values(colorsResult)) {
      if (typeof value === "string" && value.trim().startsWith("#")) {
        candidates.push(value);
      }
    }
  }

  return uniqueSwatches(candidates);
}

function buildTempFileUri(generationId: string) {
  const cacheDir = FileSystem.cacheDirectory;
  if (!cacheDir) {
    throw new Error("Cache directory is unavailable on this device");
  }
  return `${cacheDir}match-color-${generationId}-${Date.now()}.jpg`;
}

async function downloadOutputToLocalFile(generationId: string, url: string) {
  const localUri = buildTempFileUri(generationId);
  const result = await FileSystem.downloadAsync(url, localUri);
  if (!result?.uri) {
    throw new Error("Failed to download preview image");
  }
  if (result.status && result.status >= 400) {
    throw new Error(`Preview download failed (HTTP ${result.status})`);
  }
  return result.uri;
}

function formatSignedUrlForDisplay(url: string) {
  if (!url) return url;
  const join = url.includes("?") ? "&" : "?";
  return `${url}${join}cb=${Date.now()}`;
}

export default function MatchColorScreen() {
  const { accessToken } = useSupabaseSession();
  const params = useLocalSearchParams<{ generationId?: string }>();
  const { width: windowWidth } = useWindowDimensions();

  const generationId =
    typeof params.generationId === "string" ? params.generationId : "";

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [statusText, setStatusText] = useState("Loading output image...");
  const [displayImageUrl, setDisplayImageUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [localPreviewUri, setLocalPreviewUri] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [swatches, setSwatches] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [selectedSwatch, setSelectedSwatch] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [adjustmentsBySwatch, setAdjustmentsBySwatch] = useState<
    Record<string, SwatchAdjustment>
  >({});

  const tempFilesRef = useRef<string[]>([]);

  const skImage = useImage(localPreviewUri || undefined);

  const previewWidth = Math.max(220, Math.min(windowWidth - 32, 480));
  const previewHeight = useMemo(() => {
    if (!skImage) return 320;
    const width = skImage.width();
    const height = skImage.height();
    if (!width || !height) return 320;
    return Math.max(220, Math.min((previewWidth * height) / width, 520));
  }, [previewWidth, skImage]);

  const previewDrawRect = useMemo(() => {
    if (!skImage) {
      return { x: 0, y: 0, width: previewWidth, height: previewHeight };
    }

    const srcWidth = skImage.width() || previewWidth;
    const srcHeight = skImage.height() || previewHeight;
    const scale = Math.min(previewWidth / srcWidth, previewHeight / srcHeight);
    const width = srcWidth * scale;
    const height = srcHeight * scale;

    return {
      x: (previewWidth - width) / 2,
      y: (previewHeight - height) / 2,
      width,
      height,
    };
  }, [skImage, previewWidth, previewHeight]);

  const selectedTargetHsl = useMemo(() => {
    const rgb = selectedSwatch ? hexToRgb(selectedSwatch) : null;
    if (!rgb) return { h: 0, s: 0, l: 0 };
    return rgbToHsl01(rgb);
  }, [selectedSwatch]);

  const currentAdjustment = useMemo(() => {
    if (!selectedSwatch) return emptySwatchAdjustment();
    return adjustmentsBySwatch[selectedSwatch] ?? emptySwatchAdjustment();
  }, [selectedSwatch, adjustmentsBySwatch]);

  const editedSwatchCount = useMemo(
    () =>
      swatches.filter((hex) => !isZeroAdjustment(adjustmentsBySwatch[hex])).length,
    [swatches, adjustmentsBySwatch]
  );

  const shaderUniforms = useMemo(
    () => ({
      targetHsl: [selectedTargetHsl.h, selectedTargetHsl.s, selectedTargetHsl.l],
      hueShift: currentAdjustment.hueShiftDeg / 360,
      satDelta: currentAdjustment.satDeltaPct / 100,
      lightDelta: currentAdjustment.lightDeltaPct / 100,
      hueTol: HUE_TOL,
      satTol: SAT_TOL,
      lightTol: LIGHT_TOL,
      feather: FEATHER,
    }),
    [selectedTargetHsl, currentAdjustment]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      for (const uri of tempFilesRef.current) {
        FileSystem.deleteAsync(uri, { idempotent: true }).catch(() => {
          // best-effort cleanup only
        });
      }
      tempFilesRef.current = [];
    };
  }, []);

  async function fetchDownloadUrl(token: string, id: string) {
    const response = await apiFetch<DownloadUrlResponse>(`/generations/${id}/download-url`, token, {
      method: "GET",
    });
    return response.download_url;
  }

  function selectSwatch(hex: string) {
    setSelectedSwatch(hex);
    const existing = adjustmentsBySwatch[hex] ?? emptySwatchAdjustment();
    setStatusText(
      `Editing ${hex} (H ${existing.hueShiftDeg} deg, S ${existing.satDeltaPct}%, L ${existing.lightDeltaPct}%)`
    );
  }

  function updateSelectedSwatchAdjustment(
    updater: (current: SwatchAdjustment) => SwatchAdjustment
  ) {
    if (!selectedSwatch) return;

    setAdjustmentsBySwatch((prev) => {
      const current = prev[selectedSwatch] ?? emptySwatchAdjustment();
      return {
        ...prev,
        [selectedSwatch]: updater(current),
      };
    });
  }

  async function loadMatchColorData() {
    if (!accessToken || !generationId) return;

    setLoading(true);
    try {
      setStatusText("Loading generation...");
      const generation = await apiFetch<GenerationRow>(`/generations/${generationId}`, accessToken, {
        method: "GET",
      });

      if (generation.status !== "done" || !generation.output_path) {
        throw new Error(generation.error || "Output image is not ready yet");
      }

      setStatusText("Preparing editable image...");
      const signedUrl = await fetchDownloadUrl(accessToken, generationId);
      setDisplayImageUrl(formatSignedUrlForDisplay(signedUrl));

      const downloadedLocalUri = await downloadOutputToLocalFile(generationId, signedUrl);
      tempFilesRef.current.push(downloadedLocalUri);
      setLocalPreviewUri(downloadedLocalUri);

      setStatusText("Extracting prominent colors...");
      const colors = await ImageColors.getColors(downloadedLocalUri, {
        cache: true,
        key: `${generationId}-${Date.now()}`,
      } as any);

      const nextSwatches = extractSwatches(colors);
      if (!nextSwatches.length) {
        throw new Error("No prominent colors found in the output image");
      }

      setSwatches(nextSwatches);
      setAdjustmentsBySwatch((prev) => {
        const next: Record<string, SwatchAdjustment> = {};
        for (const hex of nextSwatches) {
          next[hex] = prev[hex] ?? emptySwatchAdjustment();
        }
        return next;
      });
      setSelectedSwatch((prev) =>
        prev && nextSwatches.includes(prev) ? prev : nextSwatches[0]
      );
      setStatusText("Select a swatch and adjust H/S/L sliders.");
    } catch (err) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";
      setStatusText(`Match Color failed: ${message}`);
      Alert.alert("Match Color", message);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!generationId) {
      setStatusText("Missing generation ID");
      return;
    }
    loadMatchColorData();
  }, [accessToken, generationId]);

  function resetAdjustments() {
    if (!selectedSwatch) {
      setStatusText("Select a swatch to reset.");
      return;
    }

    setAdjustmentsBySwatch((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      [selectedSwatch]: emptySwatchAdjustment(),
    }));
    setStatusText(`Reset ${selectedSwatch}`);
  }

  async function handleSave() {
    if (!accessToken || !generationId) {
      Alert.alert("Match Color", "Missing generation ID.");
      return;
    }

    const edits = swatches
      .map((hex) => ({
        hex,
        adjustment: adjustmentsBySwatch[hex] ?? emptySwatchAdjustment(),
      }))
      .filter((item) => !isZeroAdjustment(item.adjustment))
      .map((item) => ({
        selected_hex: item.hex,
        hue_shift_degrees: item.adjustment.hueShiftDeg,
        saturation_delta_percent: item.adjustment.satDeltaPct,
        lightness_delta_percent: item.adjustment.lightDeltaPct,
      }));

    if (!edits.length) {
      Alert.alert("Match Color", "Make at least one swatch adjustment before saving.");
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);
    try {
      setStatusText(`Saving ${edits.length} swatch edit(s) at full resolution...`);
      await apiFetch<MatchColorSaveResponse>(`/generations/${generationId}/match-color`, accessToken, {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({
          edits,
        }),
      });

      setStatusText("Saved. Returning to output viewer...");
      Alert.alert("Match Color", "Output image updated.");
      router.back();
    } catch (err) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";
      setStatusText(`Save failed: ${message}`);
      Alert.alert("Save failed", message);
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  const actionBusy = loading || saving;

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.safe}>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.container}>
        <View style={styles.headerRow}>
          <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
            <Text style={styles.title}>Match Color</Text>
            <Text style={styles.subtitle}>
              Select a prominent color, then adjust H/S/L for that color range
            </Text>
          </View>
          <Pressable style={styles.secondaryBtnSmall} onPress={() => router.back()}>
            <Text style={styles.secondaryBtnSmallText}>Close</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.statusCard}>
          <Text style={styles.statusText}>{statusText}</Text>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.card}>
          <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>Preview</Text>
          <View style={[styles.previewWrap, { height: previewHeight }]}>
            {loading ? (
              <View style={styles.previewCenter}>
                <ActivityIndicator color="#fff" />
                <Text style={styles.previewHelper}>Loading image...</Text>
              </View>
            ) : localPreviewUri && skImage && MATCH_COLOR_EFFECT && selectedSwatch ? (
              <Canvas style={{ width: previewWidth, height: previewHeight }}>
                <Fill>
                  <Shader source={MATCH_COLOR_EFFECT} uniforms={shaderUniforms as any}>
                    <ImageShader
                      image={skImage}
                      x={previewDrawRect.x}
                      y={previewDrawRect.y}
                      width={previewDrawRect.width}
                      height={previewDrawRect.height}
                    />
                  </Shader>
                </Fill>
              </Canvas>
            ) : localPreviewUri && skImage ? (
              <Canvas style={{ width: previewWidth, height: previewHeight }}>
                <SkiaImage
                  image={skImage}
                  x={previewDrawRect.x}
                  y={previewDrawRect.y}
                  width={previewDrawRect.width}
                  height={previewDrawRect.height}
                />
              </Canvas>
            ) : displayImageUrl ? (
              <Image
                source={{ uri: displayImageUrl }}
                style={{ width: previewWidth, height: previewHeight }}
                resizeMode="contain"
              />
            ) : (
              <View style={styles.previewCenter}>
                <Text style={styles.previewHelper}>No preview available</Text>
              </View>
            )}
          </View>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.card}>
          <View style={styles.sectionRow}>
            <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>Adjust Colors</Text>
            <Pressable
              style={[styles.secondaryBtnInline, actionBusy && styles.disabledBtn]}
              onPress={loadMatchColorData}
              disabled={actionBusy}
            >
              <Text style={styles.secondaryBtnInlineText}>
                {loading ? "Refreshing..." : "Refresh Colors"}
              </Text>
            </Pressable>
          </View>

          {swatches.length ? (
            <>
              <View style={styles.swatchWrap}>
                {swatches.map((hex) => {
                  const selected = selectedSwatch === hex;
                  const edited = !isZeroAdjustment(adjustmentsBySwatch[hex]);

                  return (
                    <Pressable
                      key={hex}
                      style={[
                        styles.swatchBtn,
                        selected && styles.swatchBtnSelected,
                        edited && styles.swatchBtnEdited,
                      ]}
                      onPress={() => selectSwatch(hex)}
                      disabled={actionBusy}
                    >
                      <View style={[styles.swatchColor, { backgroundColor: hex }]} />
                    </Pressable>
                  );
                })}
              </View>

              <View style={styles.swatchMetaRow}>
                <Text style={styles.helperText}>
                  Selected: {selectedSwatch ?? "None"} (same-color areas may also change in V1)
                </Text>
                <Text style={styles.swatchMetaPill}>
                  Edited: {editedSwatchCount}/{swatches.length}
                </Text>
              </View>
            </>
          ) : (
            <Text style={styles.helperText}>
              No swatches yet. Load the image and extract colors first.
            </Text>
          )}

          <View style={styles.sliderGroup}>
            <View style={styles.sliderLabelRow}>
              <Text style={styles.sliderLabel}>Hue</Text>
              <Text style={styles.sliderValue}>{currentAdjustment.hueShiftDeg} deg</Text>
            </View>
            <Slider
              minimumValue={-180}
              maximumValue={180}
              step={1}
              value={currentAdjustment.hueShiftDeg}
              onValueChange={(v: number) =>
                updateSelectedSwatchAdjustment((current) => ({
                  ...current,
                  hueShiftDeg: Math.round(v),
                }))
              }
              disabled={!selectedSwatch || actionBusy}
              minimumTrackTintColor="#111827"
              maximumTrackTintColor="#d1d5db"
              thumbTintColor="#111827"
            />
          </View>

          <View style={styles.sliderGroup}>
            <View style={styles.sliderLabelRow}>
              <Text style={styles.sliderLabel}>Saturation</Text>
              <Text style={styles.sliderValue}>{currentAdjustment.satDeltaPct}%</Text>
            </View>
            <Slider
              minimumValue={-100}
              maximumValue={100}
              step={1}
              value={currentAdjustment.satDeltaPct}
              onValueChange={(v: number) =>
                updateSelectedSwatchAdjustment((current) => ({
                  ...current,
                  satDeltaPct: Math.round(v),
                }))
              }
              disabled={!selectedSwatch || actionBusy}
              minimumTrackTintColor="#111827"
              maximumTrackTintColor="#d1d5db"
              thumbTintColor="#111827"
            />
          </View>

          <View style={styles.sliderGroup}>
            <View style={styles.sliderLabelRow}>
              <Text style={styles.sliderLabel}>Lightness</Text>
              <Text style={styles.sliderValue}>{currentAdjustment.lightDeltaPct}%</Text>
            </View>
            <Slider
              minimumValue={-100}
              maximumValue={100}
              step={1}
              value={currentAdjustment.lightDeltaPct}
              onValueChange={(v: number) =>
                updateSelectedSwatchAdjustment((current) => ({
                  ...current,
                  lightDeltaPct: Math.round(v),
                }))
              }
              disabled={!selectedSwatch || actionBusy}
              minimumTrackTintColor="#111827"
              maximumTrackTintColor="#d1d5db"
              thumbTintColor="#111827"
            />
          </View>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.actionsRow}>
          <Pressable
            style={[
              styles.secondaryBtn,
              (!selectedSwatch || actionBusy) && styles.disabledBtn,
            ]}
            onPress={resetAdjustments}
            disabled={!selectedSwatch || actionBusy}
          >
            <Text style={styles.secondaryBtnText}>Reset Selected</Text>
          </Pressable>

          <Pressable
            style={[
              styles.primaryBtn,
              (editedSwatchCount === 0 || actionBusy) && styles.disabledBtn,
            ]}
            onPress={handleSave}
            disabled={editedSwatchCount === 0 || actionBusy}
          >
            <Text style={styles.primaryBtnText}>{saving ? "Saving..." : "Save"}</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  safe: {
    flex: 1,
    backgroundColor: "#f5f3ee",
  },
  container: {
    padding: 16,
    paddingBottom: 28,
    gap: 12,
  },
  headerRow: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    gap: 10,
  },
  title: {
    fontSize: 24,
    fontWeight: "700",
    color: "#111827",
  },
  subtitle: {
    color: "#6b7280",
    fontSize: 12,
    lineHeight: 18,
  },
  statusCard: {
    backgroundColor: "#fff",
    borderRadius: 14,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: "#e5e7eb",
    padding: 14,
  },

=== D:\AiVastraReactJSDemo 2.2 Codex\references\mobile-backend\mobile\app\output-history.tsx ===
import { router } from "expo-router";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useFocusEffect } from "@react-navigation/native";
import {
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
  Image,
  Pressable,
  SafeAreaView,
  ScrollView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  View,
} from "react-native";

import { useSupabaseSession } from "../lib/auth";
import { apiFetch } from "../lib/backend";
import {
  buildGenerationDownloadFilename,
  downloadAndSaveImageToPhotos,
} from "../lib/downloads";

type GenerationRow = {
  id: string;
  shop_id: string;
  hero_image_id: string;
  fabric_image_id: string;
  folder_id: string;
  status: string;
  output_path: string | null;
  error: string | null;
  credits_used: number;
  created_at: string;
  started_at: string | null;
  completed_at: string | null;
};

type DownloadUrlResponse = {
  generation_id: string;
  download_url: string;
  expires_in_seconds: number;
};

type PreviewMap = Record<string, string>;

export default function OutputHistoryScreen() {
  const { accessToken } = useSupabaseSession();

  const [rows, setRows] = useState<GenerationRow[]>([]);
  const [previewUrls, setPreviewUrls] = useState<PreviewMap>({});
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [statusText, setStatusText] = useState("Loading outputs...");
  const [downloadingGenerationId, setDownloadingGenerationId] = useState<string | null>(
    null
  );

  const visibleRows = useMemo(
    () => rows.filter((row) => row.status === "done" && !!row.output_path),
    [rows]
  );

  async function getGenerationDownloadUrl(token: string, generationId: string) {
    const response = await apiFetch<DownloadUrlResponse>(
      `/generations/${generationId}/download-url`,
      token,
      { method: "GET" }
    );
    const join = response.download_url.includes("?") ? "&" : "?";
    return `${response.download_url}${join}cb=${Date.now()}`;
  }

  async function loadHistory() {
    if (!accessToken) return;

    setLoading(true);
    try {
      setPreviewUrls({});
      const generations = await apiFetch<GenerationRow[]>(
        "/generations?status=done&limit=100",
        accessToken,
        { method: "GET" }
      );

      setRows(generations);
      setStatusText(
        generations.length
          ? `Loaded ${generations.length} output image(s).`
          : "No outputs yet. Generate your first visualization."
      );
    } catch (err) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";
      setStatusText(`Failed to load output history: ${message}`);
      Alert.alert("Output History", message);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!accessToken) {
      setRows([]);
      setPreviewUrls({});
      return;
    }
    loadHistory();
  }, [accessToken]);

  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      if (!accessToken) return;
      loadHistory().catch(() => {
        // state handled in loader
      });
    }, [accessToken])
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!accessToken || !visibleRows.length) return;

    const missing = visibleRows.filter((row) => !previewUrls[row.id]);
    if (!missing.length) return;

    Promise.all(
      missing.map(async (row) => {
        try {
          const url = await getGenerationDownloadUrl(accessToken, row.id);
          return { id: row.id, url };
        } catch {
          return null;
        }
      })
    )
      .then((results) => {
        const valid = results.filter(
          (item): item is { id: string; url: string } => !!item
        );
        if (!valid.length) return;
        setPreviewUrls((prev) => {
          const next = { ...prev };
          for (const item of valid) next[item.id] = item.url;
          return next;
        });
      })
      .catch(() => {
        // best effort; user can refresh
      });
  }, [accessToken, visibleRows, previewUrls]);

  async function handleTileDownload(row: GenerationRow) {
    if (!accessToken || !row.output_path) return;

    setDownloadingGenerationId(row.id);
    setStatusText("Preparing download...");
    try {
      const freshUrl = await getGenerationDownloadUrl(accessToken, row.id);

      await downloadAndSaveImageToPhotos({
        url: freshUrl,
        filename: buildGenerationDownloadFilename(row.id, row.output_path, freshUrl),
        onStatusChange: (message) => setStatusText(message),
      });

      setStatusText("Saved to Photos");
      Alert.alert("Download complete", "Output saved to Photos.");
    } catch (err) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";
      setStatusText(`Download failed: ${message}`);
      Alert.alert("Download failed", message);
    } finally {
      setDownloadingGenerationId(null);
    }
  }

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.safe}>
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.container}>
        <View style={styles.headerRow}>
          <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
            <Text style={styles.title}>Output History</Text>
            <Text style={styles.subtitle}>All generated outputs in image tiles</Text>
          </View>
          <Pressable style={styles.secondaryBtnSmall} onPress={() => router.back()}>
            <Text style={styles.secondaryBtnSmallText}>Back</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.toolbarCard}>
          <Text style={styles.statusText}>{statusText}</Text>

          <View style={styles.toolbarRow}>
            <Pressable
              style={[styles.secondaryBtn, loading && styles.disabledBtn]}
              onPress={loadHistory}
              disabled={loading}
            >
              <Text style={styles.secondaryBtnText}>
                {loading ? "Refreshing..." : "Refresh"}
              </Text>
            </Pressable>

            <Pressable
              style={styles.primaryBtn}
              onPress={() => router.push("/carousel" as any)}
            >
              <Text style={styles.primaryBtnText}>Carousel Mode</Text>
            </Pressable>
          </View>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.gridWrap}>
          {loading ? (
            <View style={styles.emptyState}>
              <ActivityIndicator />
              <Text style={styles.emptyStateText}>Loading outputs...</Text>
            </View>
          ) : visibleRows.length === 0 ? (
            <View style={styles.emptyState}>
              <Text style={styles.emptyStateText}>No outputs yet.</Text>
              <Text style={styles.helperText}>
                Generate an image from the Visualize screen to see it here.
              </Text>
            </View>
          ) : (
            visibleRows.map((row) => {
              const previewUrl = previewUrls[row.id] ?? null;
              const downloading = downloadingGenerationId === row.id;

              return (
                <View key={row.id} style={styles.tile}>
                  <Pressable
                    style={styles.tilePressArea}
                    onPress={() =>
                      router.push({
                        pathname: "/output-viewer" as any,
                        params: {
                          generationId: row.id,
                        },
                      } as any)
                    }
                  >
                    {previewUrl ? (
                      <Image source={{ uri: previewUrl }} style={styles.tileImage} />
                    ) : (
                      <View style={styles.tilePlaceholder}>
                        <ActivityIndicator size="small" />
                      </View>
                    )}
                  </Pressable>

                  <Pressable
                    style={[styles.downloadIconBtn, downloading && styles.disabledBtn]}
                    onPress={() => handleTileDownload(row)}
                    disabled={downloading}
                  >
                    <Text style={styles.downloadIconText}>
                      {downloading ? "..." : "DL"}
                    </Text>
                  </Pressable>
                </View>
              );
            })
          )}
        </View>
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  safe: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: "#f5f3ee" },
  container: { padding: 16, paddingBottom: 28, gap: 12 },
  headerRow: { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: 10 },
  title: { fontSize: 24, fontWeight: "700", color: "#111827" },
  subtitle: { color: "#6b7280", fontSize: 12 },
  toolbarCard: {
    backgroundColor: "#fff",
    borderRadius: 14,

  },
  statusText: { color: "#6b7280", fontSize: 12 },

  primaryBtn: {
    flex: 1,

  },
  primaryBtnText: { color: "#fff", fontWeight: "700" },

=== D:\AiVastraReactJSDemo 2.2 Codex\references\mobile-backend\mobile\app\output-viewer.tsx ===
import { router, useLocalSearchParams } from "expo-router";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useFocusEffect } from "@react-navigation/native";
import {
  ActivityIndicator,
  Alert,
  Image,
  Pressable,
  SafeAreaView,
  StyleSheet,
  Text,
  View,
} from "react-native";

import { useSupabaseSession } from "../lib/auth";
import { apiFetch } from "../lib/backend";
import {
  buildGenerationDownloadFilename,
  downloadAndSaveImageToPhotos,
} from "../lib/downloads";

type GenerationRow = {
  id: string;
  status: string;
  output_path: string | null;
  error: string | null;
  created_at: string;
  completed_at: string | null;
};

type DownloadUrlResponse = {
  generation_id: string;
  download_url: string;
  expires_in_seconds: number;
};

function isPendingStatus(status?: string | null) {
  return status === "queued" || status === "processing";
}

export default function OutputViewerScreen() {
  const { accessToken } = useSupabaseSession();
  const params = useLocalSearchParams<{
    generationId?: string;
    imageUrl?: string;
    title?: string;
    mode?: string;
    refreshNonce?: string;
  }>();

  const generationId =
    typeof params.generationId === "string" ? params.generationId : "";
  const externalImageUrl =
    typeof params.imageUrl === "string" ? params.imageUrl : "";
  const externalTitle =
    typeof params.title === "string" ? params.title : "Output";
  const refreshNonce =
    typeof params.refreshNonce === "string" ? params.refreshNonce : "";

  const [generation, setGeneration] = useState<GenerationRow | null>(null);
  const [imageUrl, setImageUrl] = useState<string | null>(externalImageUrl || null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [downloading, setDownloading] = useState(false);
  const [statusText, setStatusText] = useState("Loading image...");

  const isGenerationMode = !!generationId;

  const screenTitle = useMemo(() => {
    if (isGenerationMode) return "Output Viewer";
    return externalTitle || "Image Viewer";
  }, [isGenerationMode, externalTitle]);

  function withCacheBust(url: string) {
    if (!url) return url;
    const join = url.includes("?") ? "&" : "?";
    return `${url}${join}cb=${Date.now()}`;
  }

  async function fetchDownloadUrl(token: string, id: string) {
    const response = await apiFetch<DownloadUrlResponse>(`/generations/${id}/download-url`, token, {
      method: "GET",
    });
    return withCacheBust(response.download_url);
  }

  async function loadGenerationView() {
    if (!accessToken || !generationId) return;

    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const row = await apiFetch<GenerationRow>(`/generations/${generationId}`, accessToken, {
        method: "GET",
      });
      setGeneration(row);

      if (row.status === "done" && row.output_path) {
        const signedUrl = await fetchDownloadUrl(accessToken, generationId);
        setImageUrl(signedUrl);
        setStatusText("Output ready");
      } else if (isPendingStatus(row.status)) {
        setImageUrl(null);
        setStatusText("Visualizing...");
      } else {
        setImageUrl(null);
        setStatusText(row.error || "Output not available");
      }
    } catch (err) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";
      setStatusText(`Load failed: ${message}`);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isGenerationMode) {
      setStatusText(externalImageUrl ? "Image loaded" : "No image supplied");
      return;
    }
    loadGenerationView();
  }, [accessToken, generationId, isGenerationMode, refreshNonce]);

  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      if (!isGenerationMode) return;
      loadGenerationView().catch(() => {
        // state handled in loader
      });
    }, [isGenerationMode, accessToken, generationId])
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!accessToken || !generationId) return;
    if (!isPendingStatus(generation?.status)) return;

    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      loadGenerationView().catch(() => {
        // state handled in loader
      });
    }, 4000);

    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [accessToken, generationId, generation?.status]);

  async function handleDownload() {
    if (!accessToken || !generationId || !generation?.output_path) {
      Alert.alert("Download", "Download is available only for generated outputs.");
      return;
    }

    setDownloading(true);
    setStatusText("Preparing download...");
    try {
      const freshUrl = await fetchDownloadUrl(accessToken, generationId);
      setImageUrl(freshUrl);

      await downloadAndSaveImageToPhotos({
        url: freshUrl,
        filename: buildGenerationDownloadFilename(
          generationId,
          generation.output_path,
          freshUrl
        ),
        onStatusChange: (message) => setStatusText(message),
      });

      setStatusText("Saved to Photos");
      Alert.alert("Download complete", "Output saved to Photos.");
    } catch (err) {
      const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Unknown error";
      setStatusText(`Download failed: ${message}`);
      Alert.alert("Download failed", message);
    } finally {
      setDownloading(false);
    }
  }

  function handleMatchColor() {
    if (!generationId) return;
    if (!generation || generation.status !== "done" || !generation.output_path) {
      Alert.alert("Match Color", "Output is not ready yet.");
      return;
    }

    router.push({
      pathname: "/match-color" as any,
      params: { generationId },
    } as any);
  }

  const generationReady =
    !!generation && generation.status === "done" && !!generation.output_path;

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.safe}>
      <View style={styles.container}>
        <View style={styles.headerRow}>
          <Text style={styles.title}>{screenTitle}</Text>
          <Pressable style={styles.closeBtn} onPress={() => router.back()}>
            <Text style={styles.closeBtnText}>Close</Text>
          </Pressable>
        </View>

        <Text style={styles.statusText}>{statusText}</Text>

        <View style={styles.viewerCard}>
          {loading ? (
            <View style={styles.center}>
              <ActivityIndicator color="#fff" />
              <Text style={styles.viewerHelper}>Loading...</Text>
            </View>
          ) : imageUrl ? (
            <Image source={{ uri: imageUrl }} style={styles.image} resizeMode="contain" />
          ) : (
            <View style={styles.center}>
              {isPendingStatus(generation?.status) ? <ActivityIndicator color="#fff" /> : null}
              <Text style={styles.viewerHelper}>
                {isPendingStatus(generation?.status)
                  ? "Visualizing..."
                  : generation?.error || "No image available"}
              </Text>
            </View>
          )}
        </View>

        <View style={styles.footerRow}>
          {isGenerationMode && !generationReady ? (
            <Pressable
              style={[styles.secondaryBtn, (loading || downloading) && styles.disabledBtn]}
              onPress={loadGenerationView}
              disabled={loading || downloading}
            >
              <Text style={styles.secondaryBtnText}>
                {loading ? "Refreshing..." : "Refresh"}
              </Text>
            </Pressable>
          ) : null}

          {isGenerationMode ? (
            <Pressable
              style={[styles.primaryBtn, downloading && styles.disabledBtn]}
              onPress={handleDownload}
              disabled={
                downloading ||
                !generation ||
                generation.status !== "done" ||
                !generation.output_path
              }
            >
              <Text style={styles.primaryBtnText}>
                {downloading ? "Saving..." : "Download"}
              </Text>
            </Pressable>
          ) : null}

          {isGenerationMode && generationReady ? (
            <Pressable
              style={[styles.secondaryBtn, (loading || downloading) && styles.disabledBtn]}
              onPress={handleMatchColor}
              disabled={loading || downloading}
            >
              <Text style={styles.secondaryBtnText}>Match Color</Text>
            </Pressable>
          ) : (
            <Pressable style={styles.primaryBtn} onPress={() => router.back()}>
              <Text style={styles.primaryBtnText}>Close</Text>
            </Pressable>
          )}
        </View>
      </View>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  safe: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: "#f5f3ee" },
  container: { flex: 1, padding: 14, gap: 10 },
  headerRow: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    justifyContent: "space-between",
    alignItems: "center",
    gap: 10,
  },
  title: { color: "#111827", fontSize: 22, fontWeight: "700", flex: 1 },
  closeBtn: {
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: "#d1d5db",
    borderRadius: 10,
    paddingHorizontal: 12,
    paddingVertical: 8,
    backgroundColor: "#fff",
  },
  closeBtnText: { color: "#111827", fontWeight: "600", fontSize: 12 },
  statusText: { color: "#6b7280", fontSize: 12 },
  viewerCard: {
    flex: 1,
    borderRadius: 16,
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: "#334155",
    backgroundColor: "#111827",
    overflow: "hidden",
  },
  image: {
    width: "100%",
    height: "100%",
    backgroundColor: "#111827",
  },
  center: {
    flex: 1,
    alignItems: "center",
    justifyContent: "center",
    gap: 8,
    padding: 20,
  },
  viewerHelper: {
    color: "#cbd5e1",
    textAlign: "center",
  },
  footerRow: {

import { Stack, usePathname, useRouter } from "expo-router";
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { ActivityIndicator, View } from "react-native";

import { useSupabaseSession } from "../lib/auth";

export default function RootLayout() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const { loading, session } = useSupabaseSession();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (loading) return;

    const isOnLogin = pathname === "/login";

    if (!session && !isOnLogin) {
      router.replace("/login");
      return;
    }

    if (session && isOnLogin) {
      router.replace("/(tabs)");
    }
  }, [loading, session, pathname, router]);

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <View
        style={{
          flex: 1,
          alignItems: "center",
          justifyContent: "center",
          backgroundColor: "#f5f3ee",
        }}
      >
        <ActivityIndicator size="large" />
      </View>
    );
  }

  return (
    <Stack screenOptions={{ headerShown: false }}>
      <Stack.Screen name="login" />
      <Stack.Screen name="(tabs)" />
      <Stack.Screen name="visualize" />
      <Stack.Screen name="hero-images" />
      <Stack.Screen name="hero-folder/[folderId]" />
      <Stack.Screen name="output-history" />
      <Stack.Screen name="output-viewer" />
      <Stack.Screen name="match-color" />
      <Stack.Screen name="carousel" />
      <Stack.Screen name="folders" />
      <Stack.Screen name="images" />
      <Stack.Screen name="generate" />
      <Stack.Screen name="history" />
      <Stack.Screen name="dev-upload" />
    </Stack>
  );
}

=== D:\AiVastraReactJSDemo 2.2 Codex\references\mobile-backend\mobile\lib\auth.ts ===
import { Session, User } from "@supabase/supabase-js";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

import { supabase } from "./supabase";

export function useSupabaseSession() {
  const [session, setSession] = useState<Session | null>(null);
  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    let mounted = true;

    supabase.auth.getSession().then(({ data, error }) => {
      if (!mounted) return;

      if (error) {
        setSession(null);
        setUser(null);
        setLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      setSession(data.session ?? null);
      setUser(data.session?.user ?? null);
      setLoading(false);
    });

    const { data: subscription } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange(
      (_event, nextSession) => {
        if (!mounted) return;
        setSession(nextSession ?? null);
        setUser(nextSession?.user ?? null);
        setLoading(false);
      }
    );

    return () => {
      mounted = false;
      subscription.subscription.unsubscribe();
    };
  }, []);

  return {
    session,
    user,
    loading,
    accessToken: session?.access_token ?? null,
  };
}

=== D:\AiVastraReactJSDemo 2.2 Codex\references\mobile-backend\mobile\lib\backend.ts ===
import { MOBILE_ENV } from "./env";

export const API_BASE_URL = MOBILE_ENV.apiBaseUrl;

type ErrorPayload = {
  detail?: string;
};

export async function apiFetch<T>(
  path: string,
  accessToken: string,
  init: RequestInit = {}
): Promise<T> {
  const headers = new Headers(init.headers || {});

  headers.set("Authorization", `Bearer ${accessToken}`);
  if (init.body && !headers.has("Content-Type")) {
    headers.set("Content-Type", "application/json");
  }

  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}${path}`, {
    ...init,
    headers,
  });

  const text = await response.text();

  let payload: unknown = null;
  if (text) {
    try {
      payload = JSON.parse(text);
    } catch {
      payload = text;
    }
  }

  if (!response.ok) {
    const detail =
      typeof payload === "object" &&
      payload !== null &&
      "detail" in (payload as ErrorPayload)
        ? String((payload as ErrorPayload).detail)
        : `HTTP ${response.status}`;

    throw new Error(detail);
  }

  return payload as T;
}

=== D:\AiVastraReactJSDemo 2.2 Codex\references\mobile-backend\mobile\lib\downloads.ts ===
import * as FileSystem from "expo-file-system/legacy";
import * as MediaLibrary from "expo-media-library";

type DownloadStatusHandler = (message: string) => void;

export type DownloadAndSaveImageToPhotosParams = {
  url: string;
  filename: string;
  onStatusChange?: DownloadStatusHandler;
};

export type DownloadAndSaveImageToPhotosResult = {
  assetId: string;
  localUri: string;
  filename: string;
};

function pushStatus(
  onStatusChange: DownloadStatusHandler | undefined,
  message: string
) {
  onStatusChange?.(message);
}

function sanitizeFilename(filename: string): string {
  const trimmed = (filename || "").trim();
  const safe = trimmed.replace(/[<>:"/\\|?*\u0000-\u001F]/g, "-");
  return safe || `ai-vastra-${Date.now()}.jpg`;
}

function stripQueryAndHash(value: string): string {
  return value.split("#")[0].split("?")[0];
}

function inferExtensionFromPath(value: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  if (!value) return null;
  const clean = stripQueryAndHash(value);
  const lastSegment = clean.split("/").pop() || "";
  const dot = lastSegment.lastIndexOf(".");
  if (dot <= 0 || dot === lastSegment.length - 1) return null;
  const ext = lastSegment.slice(dot + 1).toLowerCase();
  if (!/^[a-z0-9]+$/.test(ext)) return null;
  return ext;
}

export function buildGenerationDownloadFilename(
  generationId: string,
  outputPath?: string | null,
  signedUrl?: string | null
): string {
  const ext =
    inferExtensionFromPath(outputPath) ||
    inferExtensionFromPath(signedUrl) ||
    "jpg";

  return sanitizeFilename(`ai-vastra-${generationId}.${ext}`);
}

export async function ensureMediaLibraryPermission(
  onStatusChange?: DownloadStatusHandler
) {
  pushStatus(onStatusChange, "Checking Photos permission...");

  let permission = await MediaLibrary.getPermissionsAsync();
  if (!permission.granted) {
    pushStatus(onStatusChange, "Requesting Photos permission...");
    permission = await MediaLibrary.requestPermissionsAsync();
  }

  if (!permission.granted) {
    throw new Error("Photos permission denied");
  }

  return permission;
}

export async function downloadFileToCache(
  url: string,
  filename: string,
  onStatusChange?: DownloadStatusHandler
) {
  const cacheDir = FileSystem.cacheDirectory;
  if (!cacheDir) {
    throw new Error("Cache directory is unavailable on this device");
  }

  const safeFilename = sanitizeFilename(filename);
  const localUri = `${cacheDir}${Date.now()}-${safeFilename}`;

  pushStatus(onStatusChange, "Downloading image...");

  const result = await FileSystem.downloadAsync(url, localUri);
  if (!result?.uri) {
    throw new Error("Download failed: file was not saved locally");
  }

  return {
    localUri: result.uri,
    status: result.status,
  };
}

export async function saveFileToPhotos(
  localUri: string,
  onStatusChange?: DownloadStatusHandler
) {
  pushStatus(onStatusChange, "Saving to Photos...");
  const asset = await MediaLibrary.createAssetAsync(localUri);
  return asset;
}

export async function downloadAndSaveImageToPhotos(
  params: DownloadAndSaveImageToPhotosParams
): Promise<DownloadAndSaveImageToPhotosResult> {
  const { url, filename, onStatusChange } = params;

  if (!url || !url.trim()) {
    throw new Error("Download URL is missing");
  }

  const safeFilename = sanitizeFilename(filename);

  await ensureMediaLibraryPermission(onStatusChange);

  let localUri: string | null = null;

  try {
    const downloadResult = await downloadFileToCache(
      url.trim(),
      safeFilename,
      onStatusChange
    );

    localUri = downloadResult.localUri;

    if (downloadResult.status && downloadResult.status >= 400) {
      throw new Error(`Download failed (HTTP ${downloadResult.status})`);
    }

    const asset = await saveFileToPhotos(localUri, onStatusChange);

    pushStatus(onStatusChange, "Saved to Photos");

    return {
      assetId: String(asset.id),
      localUri,
      filename: safeFilename,
    };
  } finally {
    if (localUri) {
      try {
        await FileSystem.deleteAsync(localUri, { idempotent: true });
      } catch {
        // Best-effort cleanup only.
      }
    }
  }
}
